- Follow the linear path through G-06 and G-07, down the stairs, up a ladder, and past the fans. You'll see another **Materia** you can't reach, but we'll come back for that in a bit. You end up in H-01 with the cargo platform.

##### Objective: One Sunlamp Down

- Recuperate at the Bench and then head east and then north and climb the stairs.
- Clear the area of enemies, then head north to turn out a lamp.
- Backtrack south and move the platform so that you can continue south.

##### Objective: Two Sunlamps Down

- Follow the linear path east then north, clearing enemies as necessary.
- Continue east and turn off the second lamp.

##### Objective: Three Sunlamps Down

- Go down the ladder and then ride the "lift" west back to H-02. (Strictly speaking this isn't a lift because it moves horizontally instead of vertically, but we'll let that one slide).
- Go south to ride a different lift east. If necessary, you can take a quick detour W to recuperate at the Bench before taking the lift.
- Go South and then counterclockwise around the C-shaped area.
- **If you are playing in Hard Mode and already have the Elemental Materia from this area, you can skip down to the next entry in red.**
- In the SW corner of H-05, go down the ladder next to the Chest. I always have trouble remembering where this is, so here is a map.

- Head NE for a battle against 2 Queen Grashtrikes. Defeating them gives you access to the control panel to the north. Use it to move the platform ALL the way down and retrieve the **MP Up Materia** from atop the moving platform.
- Now raise the platform so that you can cross to the other side and retrieve the **Elemental Materia** you saw earlier but couldn't reach.

**Elemental Materia** is one of the most useful in the game and there are only 2 of them in the whole game, so this is a great find!

- Return S and W to the ladder at H-06 and climb up it.
- **If you are playing in Hard Mode, resume here**.
- Head east and south to another control panel.
- Move the platform down and left so that you can cross.
- Clear out the enemies, then go up the ladder to douse the third lamp. You might want to put on your sunglasses first.

##### Objective: Power for the Platform

- Head for the marker back at H-01. That's down the ladder, W across the moving platform, clockwise around the C-shaped area, then N to take the lift to the West and proceed W to the bench.
- Rest at the bench, but DON'T USE THE CARGO PLATFORM YET!

Pardon me for shouting, but if you ride out of here, you'll miss a Music Disc AND leave a Trophy behind, and I KNOW that you don't want to do either of those things.

Look at your Map, and you'll see an area to the West that you haven't visited yet. That usually means that something good is hiding there. You couldn't access this area before, but now that the lights are out and you have power, you can. So, . . .

- Ride the new lift West to the new area. Go up the ladder and into the room. Buy **Music Disc #18** from the vending machine and get prepared, because you have a challenge ahead of you.

After you interact with the console in this room, you have exactly one minute to enter the adjoining room, defeat any enemies in there, and then interact with another console to stop the timer. You may have to try this a couple of times, but you can do it, and it is worth it.

I think that the possible enemies in the room change, but on the first attempt I always get a Queen Gashtrike and 2 Sentry Rays.

- Give Cloud his Buster Sword and use its paired slots to equip your new **Elemental Materia** and **Ice Materia**. This will give his physical attacks more oomph against the Queen. Make sure that Tifa and Barret each have a **Lightning** **Materia**.
- Have Cloud attack the Queen (she's in the second alcove on the left) in Punisher Mode until he gets Berserked and has an ATB bar. Use it to cast Blizzara if he has it, Blizzard if he doesn't.
- Have Barret unload his Overcharge attack on a Sentry Ray. When he has an ATB, have him hit it with Thundara if he has it or Thunder if he doesn't.
- Send Tifa after the Queen and try to Stagger her. When she has an ATB bar, she can throw Thunder at a Sentry Ray.
- At this point, the Sentry Rays should be gone and the Queen G's health bar should be very low. Target her and finish her off, then head N and interact with the console to open the door.

If you do this correctly, it should only take about 30 seconds.

If you don't succeed on the first try, you get a much easier battle (3 Gashtrikes) on the second attempt.

- Go through the door and turn right to find the **Chocobo and Moogle Materia** that you saw in the fan earlier. This also earns you the Cleanup Crew Trophy.

| **Way of the Fist Vol. VI Manuscript**<br>When you return to this Chapter in Hard Mode, be sure to access this special area again. Defeating the enemies will again open the door. Inside you will find a chest containing the Way of the Fist Vol. VI Manuscript for Tifa. |

##### Objective: To the Power Platform

- You can now go back down the ladder and East to activate the cargo platform and ride it South to a new area.
- Your next battle is against 6 mechs:
  - Sentry Ray x 2
  - Sentry Launcher x 2
  - Sentry Gun Prototype x 2
- In general, mechs like these have relatively high Defense against physical attacks and relatively low Magic Defense. Therefore, you want to use Thunder-based Magic to do damage whenever possible. This is the last battle of the Chapter, so there's no point in conserving Magic.
- Equip everyone with **Lightning Materia** and give one team member the **Lightning**-**Elemental** pair on his/her weapon.
- The Sentry Rays and Sentry Gun Prototypes are out of reach for melee attacks.
  - Have Barret use his Overcharge on a Sentry Ray and then use the resulting ATB to throw a Thunder-based spell at the other one. His should be able to finish both of them with normal attacks.
  - Cloud and Tifa can beat up on the Sentry Gun Prototypes to build ATB, then use that ATB for Thunder-based spells.
- After the battle, just follow the path to Biggs. He will give you some gear -- including a Light Machine Gun for Barret -- and gets a big hug -- almost -- from the big guy.

Since you have a new weapon, remember to equip it, set it to Upgrade automatically, and make note of its unique ability (Lifesaver) that you should use at every opportunity until you learn it.

On to Chapter 7!

## Chapter 7 -- A Trap is Sprung

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- In this Chapter, we're going to pick up a **Music Disc** and a weapon and get a Trophy for solving a puzzle. None of these will be automatic, so let's keep a sharp lookout for them.<br>- Although somewhat convoluted, I didn't find this Chapter nearly as confusing to navigate as Chapter 6. |

#### Walkthrough:

| **Enemies with Elemental Weaknesses**<br>Here's a free tip. Since you'll be fighting a lot of mechs in this area and since Barret's new weapon has a joined pair of Materia slots, equip one of those slots with a **Lightning Materia** and the other with your new **Elemental Materia**. Now his attacks will have the Lightning elemental attached to them and will do more damage to mechs.<br>In battles against human enemies, you should replace **Lightning** with **Fire**. Of course, you can't change Materia during battle, so you're going to need to anticipate. In general, animals are weak to Ice and flying enemies are weak to Wind.<br>Equipping a Materia pair like this in your armor instead of on your weapon will cause you to resist damage from that element or, when the Materia is maxed out, can actually restore HP when your character is attacked with that element. |

##### Objective: Reach Mako Storage

- Go all the way to the SW. When you reach a dead end, Tifa will suggest a "creative" way to proceed.

- Note that you are generally traveling down here, and the location numbers getting larger (e.g., from B5 to B6). If you keep that in mind, you shouldn't have any trouble getting to the reactor.
- You're about to encounter a lot of human enemies, so this would be a good time to swap in **Fire Materia** for **Lightning Materia** on Barret's weapon.

##### Objective: Sentenced to Death

- You will need a **keycard**, which you will find conveniently glowing behind you along the W wall. Use it to access the console and dispose of an M unit.
- This is where things can get confusing. Although the checkpoint marker is South of you, you actually want to head North. This is because the marker is on a different level. When in doubt, follow Tifa and Barret. Also observe that your team is now heading upwards.
- In the room on B7, you'll find a **keycard** along the West wall and a choice of 2 ways to undermine your upcoming enemy. Really, it's your choice here. I chose to disable the AI so that it would slow down. Don't overlook the free **Lightning Materia** in the NW corner.
- Go out the South door to B6. After beating the enemies in the passageway, look in the SW corner and find a bonus **keycard**! Continue up to the door that says B6.
- Clear the enemies and collect another **keycard** from the West wall. You have 3 choices but 2 keycards. Again, this is up to you.
- Exit North and see the purple Chest ahead. Purple chests mean weapons. In this case, you get a pair of Sonic Strikers for Tifa. Equip, set to auto-upgrade, fill with Materia, and make note of the new Focused Strike ability to learn.

- Continue uphill to B5. Defeat the enemies and find **2 keycards** \-\- one along the East wall and one in the NW corner. Choose 2 of the 4 systems to flag for disposal and exit South.
- Follow the linear path to the marker at B4. Defeat the Cutter to open the way forward.

##### Objective: Storm the Gates

- When you get to the Front Gate level. Unlock and enter the room and patiently try to coordinate pushing levers with the rest of the team. This will unlock the door you need to proceed, but before proceeding you want to also unlock the door to the Disposal Room. Go back to the levers and synchronize pushing the levers 4 times. You will receive the In Lockstep Trophy. This is unbelievably difficult and frustrating, so don't get discouraged if this takes dozens of attempts.

- Enter the Disposal Room (it's the purple Discovery marker on your Map) and pull a switch. In there you will find a number of "chests" to open (for me it was 6 chests) and a rare orb of **Magic Up Materia**.
- Loop around to the North to find a bench and a vending machine that will sell you **Music Disc #2**. While you're shopping, you should buy the 3 Megapotions that are offered at sale prices. They might come in handy in the upcoming battle.
- When you're ready, exit West and then North to the marker to fight the Chapter's boss.

Airbuster isn't that hard to beat, but (going on my soapbox for a moment) I really hate it when a boss flies away to a place where you can't reach it and then bombards you with attacks. Barret, of course, can do some damage with his ranged attacks, but Cloud and Tifa can't do much because their ATB fills so slowly. Grrrrrrr! Anyway, Airbuster's defeat leads to such an explosive finish that even Tifa gets carried away, and Chapter 7 is one for the books.

Ta-da!

## Chapter 8 -- Budding Bodyguard

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- There is a LOT to do in this Chapter. We have 2 new weapons to acquire, a new Summon to get, many side quests to undertake, 5 **Music Discs** to track down, and some specific steps to take towards eventually earning the Dressed to the Nines Trophy.<br>- Be especially careful not to stray from the path I lay out for you so that you're sure not to miss anything.<br>- For my part, I will try to be especially thorough in describing your every action to help you stay on task. |

#### Walkthrough:

##### Objective: Second Chance Meeting

- Meet up with Aerith, wipe out some Shinra thugs, and you will face Reno.

##### Objective: Escape from the Church

- The path to the rooftops is linear. Just do it.
- The journey through the rooftops is easy to navigate. It's hard to get lost or miss a chest. Enjoy the fun banter between the characters. In terms of the story, this is one of my favorite parts of the game.

##### Objective: Head for the Station

Aerith comes equipped with the Guard Stick. When she joins the Party, set it to auto-upgrade and make a note to start learning her Arcane Ward ability (as described in the box below). You can also give her an Accessory.

| **Aerith's Combat Basics**<br>- As you might expect, Aerith is your best Magic user. As you might not expect, she is arguably your most potent offensive character. This is because in FFVIIR, Magic rules. She can also attack safely from a distance, which is good because her Defense is barely higher than Tifa's.<br>- Her basic offense is a 5-attack string of Magic that can be executed by pressing Square 5 times or by just holding down Square.<br>- Her Triangle attack is Tempest, and it can be executed in 2 ways -- charged or uncharged.<br>- Both versions of Tempest will regain some ATB for Aerith and will increase enemy Stagger and/or Pressure.<br>  - The uncharged version can be executed by just tapping Triangle 3 times for 3 hits of Magic. These attacks take much longer than standard Square attacks.<br>  - For the charged version, hold Triangle until a little cloud forms around Aerith's feet. Then release Triangle to launch the attack.<br>    - Some damage will occur immediately.<br>    - Additionally, a "blast perimeter" will form around the targeted enemy. After a couple of seconds, an explosion will occur and any enemies within that perimeter will be damaged.<br>    - Because of the delay involved, this charged version works best on enemies with low mobility, since highly mobile enemies can dodge the blast by simply moving outside the perimeter.<br>    - One of the super-cool things about a charged Tempest attack is that if it connects, it can restore up to 80% of an ATB bar for Aerith!<br>- Aerith begins the game with 2 ATB abilities -- Soul Drain and Arcane Ward.<br>  - Soul Drain costs one ATB bar. It will cause damage to the targeted enemy and will restore some MP to Aerith. For normal enemies, Aerith will recover 3% of her maximum MP. For Staggered enemies, that number is 15%. Soul Drain can interrupt an enemy's Magic casting that is in progress.<br>  - Arcane Ward is arguably Aerith's best ATB ability.<br>    - For a cost of 1 ATB bar, Aerith can create a "Ward" or area around any character.<br>    - Whenever any ally stands within this Ward and casts an _attacking_ Magic spell, that spell will automatically be cast twice.<br>    - **Henceforth, whenever I say something like, "Have Aerith double cast Thundaga" what I mean is "Have Aerith stand in an Arcane Ward and manually cast Thundaga once." The "double" refers to the automatic second casting that results from her being in the Arcane Ward**.<br>    - The Ward will last for a full 3 minutes.<br>    - There can only be one Arcane Ward at a time.<br>    - The Arcane Ward only works on Attack spells like Fire. Healing spells and status spells (Iike Poison) will only be cast once.<br>    - Although the second casting does not cost any additional ATB, it DOES cost some MP, contrary to what you might read elsewhere. From my experimenting, that cost appears to be about 25% of the usual MP cost.<br>    - There is a minor hitch here in that the second casting only has half the power of the first. So you're actually getting the power of 1.5 spells for 1.25 times the cost of a single spell. That is a 20% bonus in terms of power per MP or, if you prefer, about a 17% discount in terms of MP spent per unit of damage.<br>    - For example, the Thundaga spell nominally requires 22 MP. Casting it from the Arcane Ward costs 22 MP for the first casting and 6 MP (rounded up from 5.5 MP) for the second, for a total of 28 MP. That comes to 28/1.5 ≈ 19 MP per Thundaga. This is a "discount" of 3/22 ≈14% (you lose a little because of rounding). This is a far cry from half price, but still a great deal. And remember, you're getting 50% more damage for the same amount of ATB.<br>    - Overall, Arcane Ward is a great way for Aerith to get the most out of her MP and ATB. It will be especially useful when we get to Hard Mode when MP will be at a premium. |

Aerith also comes equipped with the **Refocus Materia**. Using it twice will complete a Battle Intel Report for Chadley and help lead to the Intelligence Agent Trophy. I'll give a more detailed explanation of this Materia and how to complete this Report in Chapter 12.

- Back on the ground, give some **Healing Materia** to Aerith and win some easy battles.

##### Objective: The Station

- The vending machine at the North end of the station will sell you **Music Disc #21**. This should bring your total up to 8.

##### Objective: Through the Backstreets

- After you loop around clockwise and open a gate, you might want to equip **Lightning Materia** for a battle against a Mech. Back off when you defeat it, because it might explode and cause damage.

##### Objective: Around the Gate

- When the path splits, the path to the North, as Aerith will point out, is a dead end, so go East.

Again, the path to the Slums is fairly linear. There is a puzzle or two along the way, but I'm not going to spoil your fun or insult your intelligence by telling you how to do them.

##### Objective: Slums Monitor

- You'll have time to collect stuff later. For now, watch some TV and then follow Aerith around and see how well-loved she is by all.

##### Objective: Leaf House Delivery

- When Aerith goes to get flower baskets, have Cloud go to the very top floor of the house, out the door, and then back down again.
- The flowers that you choose to pick will determine the decoration that will be on the side of Leaf House a bit later in the Chapter. The possibilities are Chocobo, Moogle, and Cactuar decorations, but I have not yet found a definitive answer to which flowers lead to which decoration. In this playthrough, I picked 2 yellow flowers and 1 white and got the Chocobo decoration, as shown in the third screenshot in this [subsection](#chapter-8----budding-bodyguard).
- Once you drop off Aerith at Leaf House you can finally explore some (and move at a decent speed again).
  - Just South of Leaf House is the community center. Inside is a jukebox from which you can get **Music Disc #25** for free.
  - While you're in there, raid the chest for the bottle of Celeris. You may want to use this in an upcoming battle.
  - Go back North and then East to the Materia dealer marked on your Map. He will sell you **Music Disc #19**. You should now have 10 Discs in your collection.
  - Just South of the Materia dealer is Chadley. Talk to him multiple times to tell him about completed Intel Reports, get new ones, and get credit for Reports that he just gave you and you already completed.
  - Chadley will also offer lots of good Materia. If a Materia goes with a completed Intel Mission, he will sell you one (and only one) for the bargain price of 100 gil. If you can buy **Wind**, **ATB Boost**, **Steal**, **First Strike**, or **Steadfast Block**for 100 gil do so.

| **Steal Materia**<br>**IMPORTANT! PLEASE READ!** \-\- Among other things, Chadley is going to assign you Intel Report 7, which is to exploit 15 enemy weaknesses. This is important because completing this Report allows you to buy the **Steal Materia**. The **Steal Materia** is essential because there is one weapon that can only be obtained by Stealing it from a boss at the end of Chapter 11. Moreover, you do not see Chadley between the end of Chapter 9 and the time that you need this Materia, so you need to buy **Steal Materia** very soon. |

It is very likely that you have already completed the conditions for this Report. However, if you haven't, you need to make this a priority. All you have to do is hit 15 different enemies with Magic to which they are weak, which you can learn by using Assess.

You can always check your progress toward completing this Report by going to Battle Intel in the Main Menu.

- Chadley also has a VR Mission to acquire Shiva, but we'll do that once Aerith is again available to help out.
- Head to the intersection just East of the marker on your Map. Somewhere around there you should find Oates, the kid who yelled at Aerith from high above earlier.

- Talk to Oates to continue the storyline. Follow him as he runs off to Leaf House.

##### Objectives: Danger in the Hideout/Escort the Children

- Oates will then lead you to the Kids' Secret Hideout. Follow him there and remember where it is (just East of Chadley). He'll then point the way to go to rescue the kids.
- Follow the almost-linear path to defeat the enemies and rescue the kids, then escort them back.

A total of 6 side quests are about to open up, and we are going to do all of them. This will help to (eventually) earn the Best in the Business Trophy and will also unlock another dress for the Dressed to the Nines Trophy.

##### Quest: The Mysterious Moogle Merchant

- Talk to the kid in the Moogle suit. Buy a membership.
- Talk to him again and buy **Music Disc #20**, a Silver Staff for Aerith, and the Graveyard Key. Spend any remaining Moogle Medals on Manuscripts, the last 4 items in his inventory. Those will give you SP for upgrading your weapons.
- Equip Aerith with her new weapon, set it to auto-upgrade, and remind yourself to start learning the Sorcerous Storm ability.

##### Quest: Kids on Patrol

- Head over to Leaf House (it's marked on your Map) and get your assignment -- to track down 5 missing kids. Ms. Folia gives you clues to their whereabouts, but here is where to find them:
  - In front of the community center, just south of Leaf House.
  - South and East of the community center, near the 3-way intersection.
  - In front of the Weapons Shop.
  - Near the Item Shop.
  - NE of town just South of the "Weapons on a Rampage" marker.

Their locations are marked on the map below. The first one described above is the furthest West, and the rest proceed counterclockwise from there.

- On your way to the community center, the Housemother (who ordered the flowers) may appear and talk to you. If she does, you should get the Say It With Flowers Trophy.

- Round up the 5 wayward brats and return to Leaf House, but you aren't done yet.
- Head back to the Hideout (near Chadley, remember?) and go to the area beyond it and take down the Hedgehog Pie King.
- Report back to the kids and get a Nail Bat for your trouble. Completing this Quest also unlocks the "A Verified Hero" Quest.

This would be a good time to check in with Chadley and take on Shiva.

| **Items Used in the Combat Simulator**<br>When you use an item in the Combat Simulator, it will be temporarily removed from your inventory. However, when you exit the Simulator, all items you used will be returned to your inventory, which seems only fair. After all, it's only a simulated battle, right? |

- Victory will earn you the **Shiva Materia** and the Summon Slayer Trophy.

| **Learning the Disorder Ability**<br>The super-slow Nail Bat is a less-than-optimal weapon for the upcoming battles against unique enemies. Even though you have a new ability to learn, I would advise sticking with Cloud's Iron Blade for now. When you get to the Colisseum in the next Chapter, you'll have a much better opportunity to use this weird excuse for a weapon enough times to learn Disorder before putting the Nail Bat on the shelf for the rest of the game. I'll remind you about this when we get there. |

##### Quest: A Verified Hero

- Squeeze back over to the kids' Hideout and talk to Oates and then Sarah.

The Whack-a-Box game is tied to a couple of Trophies. Score 10,000 points now to complete this Quest, and that should be pretty easy. Score 30,000 points, and you earn the Crate Annihilator Trophy. Later the game will be available in Hard Mode, and a score of 30,000 in Hard Mode will earn the Whack-a-Box Wunderkind Trophy. I would advise playing now until you earn the Crate Annihilator Trophy.

Be sure to read all of the rules, but I'll summarize some important points here.

| **Winning at Whack-a-Box**<br>1. Point values for boxes are marked on the boxes. Higher-point boxes are obviously going to be harder to break.<br>2. The only weapon abilities that you can use are Braver and the ability that goes with your equipped weapon. The Iron Blade with its Triple Slash ability is the logical choice.<br>3. Don't use Braver. It takes too long to execute, and it can miss. Stick with Triple Slash.<br>4. Go to the Main Menu > Battle Settings and program a shortcut for Triple Slash. This will save some time.<br>5. Don't pound the Square button. Hold it down to get the AOE attacks. This will do a much better job of destroying multiple boxes with each attack.<br>6. Seek out the small red boxes. Destroying a red box extends your time by 10 seconds.<br>7. Where you stand is important! You can waste a lot of time slashing at thin air if you don't face up to your target.<br>8. The field is divided into areas, separated by 1500-point boxes that you must whack in order to proceed. When there are 2 of these side-by-side, use attacks that will hit both. Three full rounds in Punisher Mode plus a Triple Slash should wipe out both big boxes if you are positioned properly. |

- You will get a Moogle Medal for each unsuccessful attempt to win the Crate Annihilator Trophy. Use any surplus to buy Manuscripts from the Moogle Merchant.

##### Quest: Weapons on a Rampage

- Go North to the marker to accept the Quest, then head North into Nuts 'n' Bolts Hills.
- Disregard the markers on your Map -- I don't think they are accurate. In the Scrapyard Back Alley portion of your Map, there is a dead end just above the center of the area where you will find 3 of your target enemies. Just East of there is a square-ish open area where you will find the other 2 (see maps below).

- The enemies will teleport around the area. If you are locked on to an enemy that teleports, you lose that lock and will have to lock onto the enemy again.
- At any point in time, a given Mark II Monodrive will either be vulnerable to physical attacks and immune to Magic or vice versa.
- If you see a clear sphere around one, that is a physical barrier. Hit it with Aero, then it will hold still for a few seconds and let you beat it up.
- Their elemental weakness is Wind.
- Return to talk to the Scared Man and complete the Quest. You will receive a pair of Protective Boots as a reward. **HANG ON TO THESE! YOU ARE GOING TO NEED THEM!** They protect you from Stop, they cannot be bought, and there is just one other place in the game where they can be obtained.

##### Quest: Paying Respects

- The Scared Man sends you to the Old Man in front of the Weapons Shop which is about 10 feet away. He will send you to the Graveyard, which is now marked on your Map. You will need the Graveyard Key that you bought from Moggie.

These enemies can be tough because they can put your team members to sleep and then beat up on them when they're helpless. The key to success is reducing their numbers quickly. Send Cloud in to attack and have Aerith retreat to a safe place where she can fill up her ATB gauge, set up an Arcane Ward, and unload 2 Blizzara attacks on one enemy. This should Pressure that enemy and very nearly Stagger him. Send Cloud over to Stagger that enemy with a Focused Thrust and finish it off with a Braver. Once you're down to 2 enemies, the going will be much easier. Finish them off one at a time in a similar fashion, then report back to the Scared Man.

##### Quest: Angel of the Slums

- Our good deed-doing is nearly over. Head to the last green marker and talk to the reporter who is so disreputable the even Aerith can't stand him.
- Inside the community center, talk to at least one of the 3 people with whom you can interact. Mireille will then enter and you can speak with her. She will send you back to the nosy reporter who will send you to Lookout Point which is about as far as it could possibly be from where you are now. Go there anyway.
- Beat up on the Chromogger, get the Calling Card, and head back to the Reporter who will indirectly give you your reward.

That's it for Quests. Doing all 6 will unlock Aerith's red dress for the Dressed to the Nines Trophy. Use your Map and R2 to make sure that all 6 Quests are complete. Before finishing the Chapter, let's gather a couple of other items.

- Head North towards the train station. Just before you get there, you will hear music. Speak to the Merchant to get a free copy of **Music Disc #23**, bringing your total to 12. (If you forget to pick this one up, you can still snag it in Chapter 14).
- If you feel like running ALL the way back to the church, there is an orb of **Chakra Materia** that you can pick up. It will still be there when we return to the church in Chapter 14, but suit yourself on this.

Time to head toward Aerith's house to battle Rude. Here are some notes on this battle:

##### Discovery Event: The Language of Flowers

- Follow Aerith to the garden for a scene. Grab the **MP Up Materia** glowing in the background.

On subsequent visits to Aerith's garden in other Chapters (particularly at the beginning of Chapter 14) this **MP Up Materia** may respawn. Be sure to look for it.

From here, just follow the story line and head out of town with an unexpected companion.

Wow! That was a REALLY long Chapter -- and we get to do it 2 more times. The good news is that on subsequent passes through this Chapter, we can skip some of the Quests. Collect another completion Trophy, and we're on to Chapter 9.

## Chapter 9 -- The Town That Never Sleeps

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Be prepared for another long Chapter with lots of side Quests and lots of items to collect, including 7 **Music Discs**.<br>- We are again going to do some very specific things to continue working towards our Dressed to the Nines Trophy, so pay particularly close attention and avoid going off script. Important decisions have been emphasized in **bold red**typeface.<br>- On a positive note, we will view our first set of 3 dresses and verify that we've done everything right so far (hopefully!) |

#### Walkthrough:

##### Objective: Take the Shortcut

- Go through the wreckage and use the mechanical arm in the obvious way.
- The path to the second mechanical arm is pretty linear, so I'll trust you to find your own way there.
- It should be pretty obvious that you need to move the box to the next position counterclockwise. Drop off Aerith on top of the box so that she can get the **Binding Materia**. Then pick her up again and transport her to the top so that she can kick down the ladder.

##### Objective: Working Together

- After Cloud joins Aerith, she shouts, "Cloud! Over here!" FOLLOW HER! She will lead you to a dead end that has a recovery bench and a vending machine that will sell you **Music Disc #22** (which, somehow, I managed to miss not once but TWICE).

- Your path from here isn't obvious because the next ladder down isn't marked on your Map. Here is its location.

##### Objective: Beyond the Dead End

- You'll battle some -- uh -- "colorful" characters and will eventually arrive at the third and last mechanical arm puzzle.
- See that piece of Materia? It is the one and only **Magnify Materia** in the game, and you absolutely do NOT want to miss it. It isn't a difficult puzzle, and the game pretty much tells you what to do, so if you're having trouble, just read the on-screen tips.

| **Hand-y Tip for the Third Mechanical Arm Puzzle**<br>After moving a container or dropping off Aerith, you don't have to worry about moving one arm out of the intended path for the other one. As soon as you switch arms, the one that you just finished using will automatically swing out of your way. There is little or no danger that one arm will impede the path of the other one, so, for example, once you get a container into position, you can just leave that arm where it is and switch to the other one. |

| #### Magnify Materia<br>The **Magnify Materia** is basically an "All" Materia. When it is paired with another Materia in joined slots, the effects of the other Materia are cast on all allies or enemies, depending. For example, **Magnify** \+ **Healing** will cast a healing spell on your entire party, and **Magnify** \+ **Fire** can cast a Fire spell on all enemies in range! |

##### Objective: Saying Goodbye

- Make your way to the Park. After some scenes, tell Aerith you're ready. A chocobo-drawn carriage will come through the gate, and Tifa will be inside. Watch closely for what she is wearing. If you did Chapter 3 as described, she should be wearing a black kimono, as shown below.

##### Objective: Chasing Tifa

- When you get to town, talk to the guys at the Chocobo carriage stop. BE CAREFUL! Your actions here have consequences! Please read the contents of the box below before proceeding.

| **Chapter 9 Side Quests**<br>Chapter 9 has a total of 5 Side Quests. You will always get the Burning Thighs Quest plus another pair of Quests, for a total of 3. The remaining pairs of Quests (other than Burning Thighs) are mutually exclusive. That is, you can only complete one of those pairs of Quests in any given run. The pair of Side Quests that you get will depend on your responses in certain specific situations, and they will also determine apparel outcomes for Cloud. (If that last clause doesn't make sense to you, don't worry. It will soon enough).<br>Some of your responses will assign hidden point values to a pair of Quests (a "Questline") that will be given to you by a character named Chocobo Sam, and other responses will assign points to a Questline that will be given to you by a character named Madame M. On this pass through Chapter 9, we want to make decisions that maximize points for Sam's Questline.<br>You don't have to sweat the specifics. If you follow the instructions that I give you, everything will work out. As we go, I'll show you which situations generate points and which responses will assign those points to the Questline we desire. For now, just be very careful to make the choices designated for you in **bold red typeface**. |

- When Sam asks you what Tifa looks like, choose the first response, "**She's in great shape**." (This gives Sam's Questline +1 point).

##### Objective: To Corneo's Mansion

- As you head into town, you will be intercepted by one Barker. Continue a little ways further and find the Barker who is in front of the Inn, which is almost due West of Chocobo Sam on the next North-South street over. You will travel in roughly a counterclockwise semicircle to find him.

- When he asks if you want to stay the night, tell him, "**No thanks.**" (Another +1 point for Sam's Questline).
- Head down the narrow N-S street on the West side of the Inn and look to your right. You will see a jukebox in a fenced in area where you can pick up **Music Disc #8**.

- Go West to the next North-South street and head South. You should see our old friend Johnny go racing past you. If you read what he says quickly enough, he'll give you a clue about where he is headed.

- Follow him North, West down a narrow alley, and then North again to a spot in front of the big "Supergirls" sign.

A new Discovery Event, " **Vagabond Johnny**" will show up on your Map.

- Talk to Johnny, and when prompted, respond "**Yeah**." (+1 point for Sam's Questline).
- While you're here, go South a bit then North up the stairs to a Western-themed bar. Talk to the cowgirl there to get **Music Disc #17**.

- Head South along the nearest major N-S thoroughfare. You should see Chadley on your left, so check in with him. If you do not have the **Steal Materia** yet, BUY ONE FROM HIM NOW. You will need it in Chapter 11, and I'm pretty sure that this is your last chance to buy one before then.
- If Chadley doesn't offer **Steal Materia**for sale, then you haven't completed Intel Report 7. This is going to make you miss a weapon that can only be obtained by Stealing it from a boss in Chapter 11. In a little while, you'll have a chance to fix this in the Colosseum, but if you don't, you're going to have to fix this in either Chapter Selection or Hard Mode.
- If you haven't already done so, you should buy a second orb of **First Strike Materia** now. You should have ample cash, and this is a great investment in your future. Keep both of these equipped at all times so that they level up.
- The VR Mission to acquire the Fat Chocobo Summon is now available, but we can take care of that in Chapter 13.
- Continue South on the same street, just past the next crossing street. On your left you will see a bar with a sign that says, "Yoidore." Interrupt the singer's performance and he will give you **Music Disc #29**.

- The Weapons Shop is marked on your Map, just South of your current location.
  - Buy at least one Headband. You're going to want its protection against Sleep the next time we pass through Chapter 8, if not before.
  - Purchase a Hardedge for Cloud, equip it, and set it to automatically upgrade.

- Continue South to Corneo's Mansion and talk to Leslie. Exit, have a brief scene with Johnny, and head over to Sam (marked on your Map).

##### Objective: The Trio

- When Sam asks you to choose, pick either **Heads** or **Tails**. (Either choice is +1.5 points for Sam's Questline).
- From here, go North toward Andrea Rhodea's marker in the NW. If you pass near the Item Shop marker (it's actually a pharmacy) you'll hear music and see "???" in the upper left corner of your screen. Go inside and buy **Music Disc #10**, for a total of 17.
- Continue to the marker and get there just in time to see a dejected Johnny leaving. Talk to the receptionist, then head over to Madame M's (also on your Map) again just missing Johnny.
- When asked, choose for Cloud to get the "**Poor Man's Course**." (This doesn't give any points towards Sam's Questline, but neither does it give any to Madame M's).
- Afterwards, have Aerith wait at the counter to talk to Madam M.
- When Cloud is asked about Aerith's outfit, choose "**It's alright**." (+1 point for Sam's Questline).
- Madam M will then send you to the underground Colosseum. It's marked on your Map, so head over there.

##### Objective: Underground Colosseum

- Downstairs, enter the first room on the left to prepare for battle. Exit and check the next room on the left for a scene with Johnny.
- In the room with Johnny, there is a small bottle on the table with which you can interact. This time through, you can choose "**I'll taste it**" if you wish (+1 point for Sam's Questline). In your subsequent runs through this Chapter, though, just ignore the bottle.
- You will fight a total of 4 rounds. Your enemies (and their weaknesses) are dogs (Ice), humans (Fire), machines (Lightning) and a House (for which you want to have all 4 elemental Materia equipped). Just do your best and try not to get yourselves killed.
- Always use the Bench to recover between rounds.
- Only the last round should give you any trouble, so let's take a closer look at that encounter.

- When finished, though, **DO NOT LEAVE THE COLOSSEUM YET**!
- Back upstairs, the Gatekeeper will offer you the chance to do some more fights. We are going to do 2 of them now. The next box will explain why.

| **Colosseum Challenges**<br>Completing all of the Colosseum challenges is one of the requirements for earning the Ultimate Weapon Trophy. You can do these later, but doing them now earns tangible rewards:<br>- Winning the solo Challenges vs. Wild Animals will earn Level 2 Limit Breaks for both Cloud and Aerith.<br>- Winning the solo Challenges vs. Shinra Thugs will earn the Vol. II Manuscripts for both Cloud and Aerith. Manuscripts are explained in more detail [here](#skill-points-and-weapon-upgrades), but in a nutshell earning a Manuscript earns Skill Points which are used to upgrade weapons.<br>Two of these challenges require that Aerith fight solo, and this is the last time that you will have access to both Aerith and the Colosseum before postgame. Unfortunately, only one of these 2 challenges -- Aerith vs. Wild Animals -- is available now. The good news is that winning it will award Aerith her Level 2 Limit Break, Planet's Protection, which makes your team immune to physical attacks for 80 seconds. For that alone, it is worth fighting these 5 consecutive battles now. so, . . . |

- Choose Easy Mode and take on the Aerith vs. Wild Animals challenge. Equip her weapon with the**Elemental**-**Ice** pair and make sure she also has **Wind** Materia for the Lesser Drake in the last round. It should take you about a minute to complete all 5 battles. Completing this challenge will earn you the Returning Champion Trophy.

- If Aerith hasn't learned her Sorcerous Storm ability, this is a good place to learn it. Just keep repeating this challenge and using the ability as often as possible until she has 100% proficiency.
- Then have Cloud do the "Cloud vs. Wild Animals" challenge. His reward will be his Level 2 Limit Break, Ascension.
- If Cloud hasn't learned the Disorder ability yet, equip him with the Nail Bat and keep winning this challenge in Easy Mode until he learns the ability. Remember that you get the Proficiency Bonus for just using a Square button attack after hitting the enemy with Disorder.

| **Limit Breaks**<br>- Limit Breaks are extra-powerful actions that can be taken by a character once that character's Limit Gauge is full.<br>- Aerith's Limit Breaks focus on healing and protection. The other 3 characters' Limits are powerful attacks.<br>- Limit Gauges fill when a character takes damage or Staggers an enemy. There are Accessories that provide other mechanisms for filling Limit Gauges. Three examples are:<br>  - Transference Module (Reward for earning 30,000 points in Whack-a-Box in Hard Mode in Chapter 14). This accessory fills the Limit Gauge slightly each time the character who has it equipped performs and ATB action.<br>  - Spectral Cogwheel (Reward for earning 30,000 points in Whack-a-Box in Chapter 8). This accessory fills the Limit Gauge slightly each time the character who has it equipped uses MP).<br>  - Götterdämmerung (Reward for defeating the Top Secrets in the Shinra Simulator). This is the most prized (and difficult to obtain) accessory in the game. It bestows its wearer with a full Limit Gauge at the start of every battle (!) and slowly recharges that gauge over time in addition to allowing charging by the usual means. We'll address getting this in [a later section of this guide](#on-to-the-shinra-combat-simulator).<br>- Each character will acquire 2 Limit Breaks, but will only have access to one at a time.<br>- To set the Limit Break of your choice, go to the Main Menu and select Battle Settings > \[Character's name\] > Limit Break.<br>- Level 2 Limit Breaks -- like the ones that you just got for Cloud and Aerith -- are generally more powerful, but their Limit Gauges also will take about 50% longer to fill.<br>- Executing a Limit Break does not require ATB, and a Limit Break can be saved until needed within the battle in which it was earned.<br>- A full Limit Gauge does not automatically empty when a character is KO'ed. That Limit Break will still be available after the character is revived.<br>- Limit Breaks do expire when a battle ends, i.e., you cannot carry one over to another battle.<br>- Limit Breaks do not stop an enemy's actions and therefore are not guaranteed to connect. Enemies can dodge them, KO the character performing them, morph into a form that is resistant to physical attacks, or disappear. Therefore, it is good to be sure that your enemy is pinned down before executing this valuable attack. |

- If you have not yet completed Battle Intel Report 7, this is your last chance before Chapter 11. Choose the "Two-Person Team Vs. Slum Outlaws" Challenge. All enemies in all 5 battles are weak against Fire. Cast Fire spells on enemies until you see a message that you have done this 15 out of 15 times. You can double-check your progress in the Main Menu.
- Head back upstairs and go to Madame M's. The choices you made in this Chapter gave 6.5 points towards Sam's Questline and no points toward Madame M's. Therefore, Madame M will send you off to see Sam, who will assign you 2 new Quests to pursue while Madame M works on Aerith.

#### Objective: Parting Ways

##### Quest: Burning Thighs

- Head over to the gym (marked on your Map) to talk to Jules. He will set you up against Ronnie.

The main thing to remember here is to never go too fast. If you do, you'll fall down and get hopelessly behind. Since the pace accelerates as you progress, this is only really a problem in the first few seconds. If you go too slowly, you'll be prompted to pound on the X button several times to get going again, but you can survive this. If you don't fall, you will win this easily.

Beating Ronnie will complete the Quest, but don't stop there. While you're in the groove, challenge and defeat Jay for an orb of **Luck Up Materia**. Then challenge Jules. When you defeat him, you will win a Champion Belt and the Sultan of Squat Trophy will be yours.

This is a really easy Trophy to win, so I would definitely advise getting it now.

##### Quest: The Party Never Stops

- Head to the Clothing Store just North of the gym and take on the seemingly endless "The Party Never Stops" Quest.

You will notice that there are ELEVEN tasks to perform to complete this Quest. On the plus side, this is just a bunch of running around talking to people. The locations will be marked on your Map and, best of all, Johnny is going to be your guide and companion.

The only sketchy part is when the Pharmacist sends you out to make deliveries. You only have to make one, and you'll likely only be given 2, but I'll give you all of the locations to save some time.

- The antiemetic goes to the guy in the bathroom at the bar where you talked to the Clothing Store clerk's father. There's a picture of this location just above the description of Cloud's Hardedge.
- The second delivery (bottle of deodorizing tablets) goes to the guy in the alley behind the Clothing Store.

- There is a long North-South alley just south of Madam M's. For the last delivery (antacid), go there and turn right (South) at the fork.

Now just follow the markers to complete the Quest.

##### Quest: A Dynamite Body

- Go back to Sam. He sends you to the Colosseum. Choose the "Special Match." Beat a pair of Bombs to complete this Quest and earn an Arcane Scepter for Aerith.

| **Check Before Proceeding**<br>Unlocking the item that we want on this pass through Chapter 9 depends on 2 things: getting Sam's Questline and completing all 3 Quests. If you do not complete all 3 Quests, you will not get the item that we seek. Therefore, before proceeding, you should check Map > R2 to make sure that all 3 Quests register as complete. |

##### Objective: Rescue Tifa

- Return to Madam M's. Johnny will drag you back to Corneo's. Go inside and talk to Leslie, then head back North. You should encounter Aerith, and if you followed instructions in Chapter 8, she should be wearing the stunning red dress pictured at the top of this page.

##### Objective: Aerith's Plan

- Aerith will lead you to the Honeybee Inn. Talk to the receptionist and go through the door, but do a Manual Save before talking to anyone inside.

A Music Disc and a Trophy are tied to the next sequence of events. Unfortunately, it is very difficult to get both in one playthrough. It will probably take several tries to get each item, and there is no opportunity to Save in between them. I'm going to suggest that we get the Music Disc for now and get the Trophy when we return here postgame. I had you manually Save in case you want to try for both at once.

Both events involve pressing correct buttons at correct times to the music. You can tell how well you do with each press by listening carefully. A Great will make a bright, sharp "Ching." A Good will be similar but less bright, and a Bad will sound, well, bad.

- Talk to the Honeybees at the door. When asked if you want to head to the Stage, choose "Yes." They will offer to let you practice first. Keep trying the practice sessions until you get 10 "Greats." If you fall short, just take the option to try again. When you do this perfectly, you will receive **Music Disc #26**. This shouldn't take more than 5 - 10 minutes.

Cloud will get dragged (almost literally, as it turns out) inside to do a similar mini-game with Andrea. As I mentioned earlier, the standard for winning the Dancing Queen Trophy is uncertain, but analysis in the box below seems reasonable.

I will leave it up to you to decide how much time you want to spend trying to win it now, but unless you nail this on the first try, it can be done much more efficiently the next time we pass through here.

- After the makeover, watch carefully for Cloud to exit. He is roughly the 9th person out the door. Follow him and carefully check out his dress. If you followed instructions correctly here in Chapter 9, he should be wearing a royal blue and black dress and have pink ribbons in his braids as shown in the picture below and at the top of this page.

We have now unlocked 3 of the 9 dresses for the appropriately named Dressed to the Nines Trophy. You should get the Snappy Dresser Trophy somewhere around here for doing so. We will unlock 3 more in each of our next 2 passes through Chapters 3, 8, and 9 by making different decisions.

##### Objective: The Audition

- Head back South to the Corneo Mansion. When the team is reunited, check the vending machine made in Corneo's image. It will sell you **Music Disc #11**.

##### Objective: Showdown with Corneo

- Before Aerith and Tifa go to "rescue" Cloud, send them downstairs to fight a trio of Corneo's goons. After the battle, squeeze through the narrow passageway under the stairs to retrieve a **Fury Ring**.

Don't forget that you just acquired a new weapon for Aerith. You should know what to do by now.

Okay -- you should be able to follow the story line the rest of the way through this long and eventful Chapter. See you in the sewers!

## Chapter 10 -- Rough Waters

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- This is a much more straightforward Chapter. We just have one Music Disc and one weapon to pick up, and no side Quests.<br>- Focus on learning weapon abilities and fulfilling Intel Report requirements.<br>- Most of the path is pretty linear, so I'm probably going to give a minimum of direction. Have some fun exploring for a change! |

#### Walkthrough:

##### Objective: Escape to the Surface

- You begin this Chapter with Tifa and Aerith lying on the ground. For reasons that I will explain when we get to Chapter 14, I am going to advise you to **walk over to Aerith and interact with her** instead of Tifa.
- Next up is a fight against a Boss who is weak against Fire.

##### Objective: Follow the Waterway

- After throwing the first switch to drain an aqueduct, the ladies will head up the stairs. However, if you head down instead, you will see a purple chest containing a set of Feathered Gloves for Tifa.

- Climb the ladder and meet up with the ladies. Enter a room with a recovery bench and a vending machine that will sell you **Music Disc #9**.
- From the NW corner of Aqueduct 2, you can either go N or E. The path to the E is a dead end, so go up the ladder to the N.
- At the N end of the Settling Basin Corridor, you want to go N, but you encounter a locked door.

- Go to the room just S of the locked door and turn on the generator.
- Go around the loop to the S, throwing the switches and exploring all of the areas.
  - On one side, you'll find some cash, giving new meaning to the phrase "filthy lucre".
  - On the other side, you'll find the Key to the Sewers, which will open the way forward.

##### Objective: Crossing the Trunk Line

- After the cut scene, look NW across the empty canal, and you'll see a piece of **Materia**. Look at your Map and make note of its location. We're going that way, but it's easy to miss if you're not paying attention.

- Before tiptoeing across the closed gate to your left, climb the short set of stairs to your right and curve around to the right get an orb of **Poison Materia**. We've already seen how useful this stuff can be.

##### Objective: To the Sector 7 Slums

- At the N end of Aqueduct 3, you'll come to a healing bench.
- You get to a room where you can go South or West. South is a Chest guarded by 5 Wererats. West and up the ladder is the path forward.

- You will go up a ladder and come to a T-intersection.

- Take a left and follow the only path there is. After descending the ladder, go North and pick up the **Warding Materia** glowing at you. **BE SURE TO GET THIS!** There are only 2 of them in the game.

- The counterclockwise loop to the South is a dead end that contains an unnecessary battle and no useful items, so double back, climb the ladder, and continue to the North and West. Eventually, you'll come to a pool of slime and a Control Room.

##### Objective: To the Surface

- After the annoying pump game, you fight a sequence of 3 battles. The last is against 3 Sahagins, and it can be tough. Use Fire Magic every chance you get and focus on one enemy at a time, and you should make a clean getaway. Well, considering where you've been, maybe it isn't so clean. The point is that you've escaped the sewers alive.

I hope that this was enough guidance for you. I tried to let you enjoy finding your own way since it's pretty hard to get lost. After a tough boss at the beginning, this Chapter hopefully went pretty smoothly. And isn't it great to smell fresh air again?

## Chapter 11 -- Haunted

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- You're going to pick up one more Music Disc and 2 weapons in this Chapter. However, as I warned you earlier, to get one of those weapons, you must have **Steal Materia**. If you miss it now, you can pick it up later, but, as is often the case in life, earlier is better.<br>- Although with rare exceptions this Chapter is fairly linear, the multiple ladders and stairs make it difficult to navigate using your map alone. So, I will again break with tradition and give detailed instructions for getting through this area. However, I would encourage you to try to navigate on your own without my help. To make sure that you don't miss any essential items, I will use **bold red font** to emphasize crucial steps that lead you to these 4 items.. |

#### Walkthrough:

##### Objective: A Train Wreck

- After descending to the ground, you will head North through one train car and East into an open area.
- Before climbing the ladder to the N, continue East through another train car and into an open area just North of the resting bench. **Go all the way South to find a purple Chest containing a Mythril Rod for Aerith** as shown above.

- As illustrated above, the Mythril Rod comes with 3 Materia slots, but none of them are paired. I suggest that you manually spend 20 AP to buy the 2 "New Materia Slot" skills in the first Core of this weapon. The first will add a slot, and the second will join 2 slots. Now Aerith can continue to use the **Magnify**-**Healing** combination.
- Go back through the train car and climb the ladder to the NE. Cross the car, then descend another ladder to arrive in the NE section of Section C facing a door.

- Go through the door, then continue N and then W into Section B. Squeeze through a narrow passage.
- After receiving a mysterious message and fighting a battle, the girls will lead you up a ladder to the NE. Go up and then descend the ladder on the other side.
- Now look at your Map. **In Switchyard 2 - Section B, there is a horizontal branch at the north end of the Map that dead ends to the West.****Go there to retrieve a****HP Up Materia**.

- Backtrack E and head N up another ladder. Follow the path N, take a, um, shortcut I guess and receive an unexpected invitation. Escort the ladies into the Maintenance Facitity.

##### Objective -- Pass Through the Maintenance Facility

- Head towards the marker at the N end. When your path becomes blocked, head back towards the door at the S end. That door will shut but another one to the W will open, so follow that path.
- You would really like to continue N through this car, but unfortunately that isn't an option. Make a mental note that your goal for most of this Chapter will be finding a way N through this car.
- For now, proceed W and climb the stairs.
- Once you are upstairs, you will be invited to enter a door to the NW, but unfortunately it is locked.

- Take the path to the E instead. There you will find a door and some stairs that go down.
  - Go downstairs first to fight a battle and claim a treasure.
  - Then head back upstairs and through the door.

- Pass through the room to the next area and enter an unlocked door to the NW.
- This room has exits to the N and the S. Go S first.
- This room has exits to the S and the E. The S exit is the locked door that you encountered earlier, so unlock it but then go back into the room and take the E exit to claim a treasure.
- Return to the room to the W, then head N and then N again to the NW corner of the Facility.
- Here you will find stairs going up, stairs going down, and a new path to the E. The stairs that go up will take you to a locked door that you cannot open yet, and the path to the E is a dead end. Your only choice is to go down the stairs.
- Downstairs you will find a ladder. Climb it, and it will take you to an overhead path to the NE part of the Facility. Descend the ladder on the E side of that path and climb the nearby stairs to a recovery bench and vending machine. **Here you can buy Music Disc #6**, bringing your count up to 21.
- Enter the door and interact with the console. This will initiate a battle with a Ghoul.

##### Objective: Shadow in the Control Room

- From here you need to backtrack to the SW portion of the Maintenance Facility (where the "Crane Game" marker is) to throw a switch. Just head through the previously-locked door on the W side of the room, go down the stairs to the W, and head S until you arrive at the marker.
- Go down the stairs to the W, then head E to enter the train car. Go N, exit to the W, go N again, and follow the path N to the marker. You can now rest at the nearby bench and then exit the facility.

##### Objective: Back Amongst Friends

- Go to the control panel and throw the switch. You will then fight a battle. Afterwards, you can heal for free by just returning to the previous Bench.
- You can now head N, climb a ladder, and then climb down another ladder.
- Climb the short ladder to enter the nearby train cabin and throw a switch. You can now climb a ladder almost directly N of there. Follow the path and slide down the one-way ladder at the other end.

This last area is not easy to navigate. Use the map below and the corresponding numbers in the text to help you find your way.

- Enter a train car to the N and follow it NE. There will be another train engine just N of you (1). Climb up and throw another switch. This will give you access to the area to the W with the 2 Drakes flying around (2)
- After defeating the Drakes, loop around clockwise to the NW into the NW part of Section A.
- Climb into the cabin of one last train to the N (3) and throw a switch. this will connect the overhead route to a new area to the N. Climb one last ladder to the E (4).

- Follow the path to the bench and vending machine (5). You should heal up and prepare for the upcoming boss battle.
- Equip both Ice and Wind Magic on as many characters as possible. Then please read the next several paragraphs before proceeding.

First, as previously mentioned, this boss has a weapon for Aerith, but you can only acquire it if you have **Steal Materia**. Hopefully, you do. If so, equip the **Steal Materia** on someone and have that character Steal every turn until you get the Bladed Staff.

You can now head to the marker. Thus endeth Chapter 11, and we are on to Chapter 12.

## Chapter 12 -- Fight for Survival

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- This is a fairly short Chapter with no Trophy-related items to collect or actions to perform. You're pretty much fighting your way along a linear path towards a pretty hairy fight against the 2 Turks.<br>- Stay focused on battle tactics and try not to just mash the Square button and you should sail through this one. |

#### Walkthrough:

Aside from some shifts in focus among the characters, this Chapter is primarily a climb up to the top of a tower. On each floor there is only one way to continue going up, and all of these ladders and stairs are clearly marked on your Map. So, on each floor you just need to clear out all enemies, search for Treasures and Events, then look at your Map for arrows pointing upwards to find the way up to the next floor. Ignore the marker showing your destination. Your destination is on the top floor, and before you get up there, this marker will only mislead you.

Since your path is fairly uncomplicated, I'm going to leave most of the navigating up to you. I'll only provide advice that might not be obvious to you on your first run through this Chapter.

##### Objective: Head for the Pillar

A bunch of spectres will block your path. Attack the Mysterious Spectres until you get a message that the Enigmatic Spectre is vulnerable. Then focus your attacks on him.

##### Objective: Link Up with Avalanche

- Proceed upwards, defeating everything in your path (how's THAT for helpful advice?)

##### Objective: Saving Marlene

- Help Aerith navigate to Seventh Heaven. Not much to actually do here.

##### Objective: To the Top

- When you get to Pillar - 12F, don't overlook the Elixir in a chest at about 1:00 on the circle.
- There is a Bench on 14F, but it's kind of hard to miss.
- Right after you join up with Barret, Reno and Rude will appear in their helicopter. When this happens, look in the lower left corner for a purple Square button labeled "Menu." Press and hold Square so that you can adjust Materia and equipment before the upcoming boss battle.

- Once the timer starts counting down, Cloud needs to go counterclockwise around the area to get to Tifa for a scene.

Once you win this battle, you're on to Chapter 13.

## Chapter 13 -- A Broken World

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- We have two **Music Discs** and 2 weapons to pick up here, and one of the weapons will come automatically. We'll also take care of a couple of Intel Reports when we see Chadley.<br>- Most of this Chapter is pretty linear, so I'm going to leave much of the navigation up to you. Use your Map to help find your way and locate hidden areas. |

#### Walkthrough:

##### Objective:Gift of Hope

- Even though Barret is rightfully in a hurry to check up on Marlene, touch base with Chadley when you get to Sector 5 Slums Area. Turn in completed Reports and, if you haven't already done so, get a **First Strike Materia**or two.
- If you used you completed Battle Intel Report 13, Chadley will sell you the one and only **ATB Assist Materia** in the game for a measly 100 gil.

Equip any **First Strike Materia** you have on someone and keep it equipped continuously until it maxes out. Leveling up the **First Strike Materia** is a top priority for the immediate future.

- We can also complete Reports 10 and 12 right now by Staggering Fat Chocobo (a VR Mission) to 200% before defeating him. You can also win the Staggering Feat Trophy by getting his Stagger gauge up to 300%, but that will be easier to do once we get a different pair of gloves for Tifa.

The Leviathan VR Mission should be available now. Winning this now is possible, so go ahead and try it if you'd like. You will have opportunities to try it in Chapters 14 and 17 and in postgame play, and frankly you'll be better prepared then. There is little advantage in winning this battle now, so I would advise postponing this until later when it will be less frustrating. I'll give some general advice on this battle in [a future section of this guide.](#leveling-up-your-materia-and-characters)

- Continue W to Aerith's house where Barret learns that you don't mess with Elmyra.

##### Objective: Checking on Friends

- Head east through the Central District of Sector 6 and then SE until you get to the entrance to the Collapsed Expressway on your left.
- Proceed through the area just like you did with Aerith, except this time you don't have to lower the ladders, which will save you some time.
- You'll get to a place where the path you took with Aerith is blocked. Cloud will identify an alternate route (see Map below).

- In the NE corner of the Bandit's Den area, be sure to follow the dead end to the SE to get a free Heavy Duty Bracer.

- When you get to Evergreen Park, check your Map and you will see there is an Item "Shop" and a Weapons "Shop."
  - The Item Shop will sell you **Music Disc #15.**
  - For 9600 gil, the Weapons Merchant will sell you 2 more Heavy-Duty Bracers for your other teammates. Your choice on this one.
  - The Weapons Merchant will sell you a Big Bertha for Barret, which you definitely want to buy.
- Make your purchases and head underground.

##### Objective: In Solitude

- Before Barret first blasts through a section of rock, there is a healing bench that you probably don't need to his right.
- You're going to control Barret solo for a while.
  - This is a great time to learn his new Maximum Fury ability. You get the Proficiency Bonus if you just kill off an enemy with this ability, which is pretty easy to do against the feeble Bugaboos in this area.
  - Also, if you haven't completed your Intel Report on Refocus yet, you can easily do it here. If you don't remember how, the process is detailed in [the previous section on Chapter 12](#chapter-12----fight-for-survival).
  - At this point, you should have 16 weapons and somewhere from 13-15 of the 16 Weapon Abilities you need to complete Intel Report 17, depending on how many Aerith learned before leaving us.

##### Objective: Finding Wedge

- NW of the place where you shot out 3 fans, you will find 2 locked rooms that Barret can shoot open. The room to the NE contains an orb of **Ice Materia** you should probably grab, if only on general principles.
- Just upstairs and on your right is another locked cell with the second and final orb of **Warding Materia**.

- Eventually, you will arrive at a bench and vending machine. Here you can purchase **Music Disc #14**, bringing your total to 23.
- Read the info in the box below, and use this time to prepare your team for the upcoming battle.

##### Boss Fight: Failed Experiment

As you may have guessed, you have a boss fight coming up.

- Kick the Failed Experiment's behind and you will get Tifa's Mythril Claws for your trouble.

- Follow the linear path to the end of the Chapter and collect another Trophy.

Again, I really hope that I gave you enough information to find your way through this Chapter.

## Chapter 14 -- In Search of Hope

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Basically, Chapter 14 is a set of 9 Quests, some of which overlap. There's a lot of running around to do, and you'll also have a chance to check some other Trophy-related tasks off your to-do list before going irreversibly over the wall and making your way to the Shinra Building.<br>- This is your last chance to do a lot of things before postgame play, so I would advise you to be patient and take care of the tasks that you can right now.<br>- In light of the above, I'll be giving much more specific guidance than in the previous couple of Chapters. I don't know whether you welcome that or dread it, but it will be there if you need it. |

#### Walkthrough:

- It isn't altogether obvious, but after Cloud comes downstairs, he needs to go left to the garden.

| **Chapter 14 Resolutions**<br>In the garden, Cloud will share a scene with one of the other 3 main characters. Actions taken in earlier Chapters will determine which character shares the scene with Cloud. There is no Trophy or other recognition for unlocking all 3 of the scenes, but it does show up in the Play Log of your progress in the game. If you follow the instructions in this guide, you should see all 3 of them.<br>I have found little agreement among Internet articles regarding the precise conditions for unlocking each of the 3 possible scenes. However, based on my research and gameplay, the following information seems to be the most plausible and accurate.<br>Essentially, the scene that is unlocked depends on hidden "affinity points" that are assigned to either Tifa or Aerith. These points are based on side quests completed and Cloud's choice at the beginning of Chapter 10, according to rules 1 - 3 below:<br>1. Every side quest completed in Chapter 3 is worth +2 points for Tifa.<br>2. Every side quest completed in Chapter 8 is worth +2 points for Aerith.<br>3. At the beginning of Chapter 10, whichever girl Cloud talks to first gets +1 point.<br>4. If Tifa has at least 6 points and more points than Aerith, then we get her scene.<br>5. If Aerith has at least 6 points and more points than Tifa, then we get her scene.<br>6. If both Tifa and Aerith have fewer than 6 points, then we get Barret's scene.<br>I am about 99% confident that rules 1 - 3 above are correct and complete, and I'm about 90% confident that statements 4 - 6 are correct. Replaying these Chapters takes SO long, that I just haven't done the work to confirm all of the possibilities.<br>Keep in mind that the points assigned to Tifa and/or Aerith are not final until you complete the necessary Chapters, e.g., Tifa's points are finalized when you complete Chapter 3.<br>If you followed my advice thus far, you should get Aerith's scene this time through (as pictured above). This is because we completed 6 side quests in both Chapter 3 and Chapter 8, and talking with Aerith at the beginning of Chapter 10 broke this 12 - 12 tie. Next time we will make choices so that we get Tifa's scene, and on our third pass, we will get Barret's.<br>If this doesn't work out for you, I would advise against trying to fix this before you have earned the Dressed to the Nines Trophy. Remember that earning all of the Trophies is our primary goal here, and you don't want to do anything to undermine that. |

##### Objective: Intel Gathering

- After the scene, the orb of **MP Up Materia** in the garden (shown above) may or may not be there. If it's there, pick it up. If it isn't, no biggie. We can get these later.
- As you head towards the Center District, you will pass an orb of **Pedometer Materia.** Pick it upand equip it on someone immediately.

Once you log 5000 (roughly 3.7 for you math nerds) steps with the **Pedometer Materia** equipped, it will turn into the one and only **AP Up Materia** in the game. As the name implies, an orb of Materia that is paired with the **AP Up Materia** earns double AP and therefore grows twice as fast.

- When you arrive in town, you'll get drawn to the rantings of [a familiar character from Chapter 3](#chapter-3----home-sweet-slum) (pictured above).
- Check in with Chadley. If you have completed Report 16, you can buy the **Enemy Skill Materia** for a measly 100 gil. You will need it for a Trophy, but we won't complete that one for a while. Even so, buy it now if you can and you can learn 3 of the 4 enemy skills as we go.

As far as the Quests go, the order of completing them isn't that important. You can save some time and running around by working on multiple Quests simultaneously, but I like to focus on one task at a time. As a bonus, it will add to our Pedometer count. Feel free to adjust the sequence of steps as you see fit.

##### Quest: Missing Children

- Ms. Folia is right across the street, and she seems to have lost track of the kids again. Fortunately, this time they are all in one place.
- Head north, and the Stablehand will stop you and give you the Missing Chocobos Quest and some Gysahl Greens. We'll get to his needs soon enough.
- For now, take a right at the fork and another right into the Slum Public Cemetery.
- DO A MANUAL SAVE before going through the gate! You are going to try to acquire an enemy skill, and this way if you fail to do so, you can reload and try again.

| **Time Saver Alert**<br>There are only 4 learnable Enemy Skills in the game. Learning all 4 will earn you the Master of Mimicry Trophy. We can acquire one of them here, and this will keep us from having to go back and do this in Chapter Selection. |

##### Quest: Chocobo Search

We might as well take this one on since we've initiated it anyway. You have 3 missing birds to track down.

Remember to use Tifa's new Chi Trap ability at every opportunity until you learn it. Notice that you get the Proficiency Bonus for just hitting an enemy after using the Chi Trap on them.

- **CHOCOBO #1**: Backtrack to the Y intersection ("Y" not?) and head north past the Station towards Sanctuary Way. You'll see the first Chocobo on your left. Interact with it, and Cloud will automatically feed it and send it on its way. One down, two to go.
- **CHOCOBO #2:** Backtrack to the Station and take the East road towards Nuts 'n' Bolts Hills. Go right when the road splits, then slide down the ladder. You will see Chocobo #2, but will have to win a battle to liberate it.

Before tracking down the last Chocobo, we can get another task out of the way towards the Master of Mimicry Trophy.

- Go a bit East to the "bird" marker. Equip Cloud with the **Enemy Skill Materia** and DO A MANUAL SAVE because this one doesn't always work.

In the area just East of here is a Smogger.

We want to acquire its Self-Destruct ability, which Smogger may or may not execute when you defeat it. I have tried many times to acquire this ability, and my success rate is about 30%. I can't explain why it works some times but doesn't work other times. I have read that you won't get the ability if you Stagger Smogger, but that just isn't true because I know that I have learned the skill after Staggering Smogger. I have read that Self-Destruct has to KO Cloud, but that isn't true either because I have learned the ability when Cloud has survived. So, I'm going to suggest that you just keep trying. Avoid finishing Smogger with a Stagger (it's okay to Stagger him during the battle) and be sure that Cloud is standing on the pieces of Smogger when he explodes. If you get the ability, great. If not, just try again. It really doesn't take long.

- **CHOCOBO #3:** Now go back west to the "bird" marker. Our next Chocobo is in the Collapsed Expressway, and for 300 gil you can get a ride to the entrance. You can try to find your way there by foot, but me, I'm riding over there in style and conserving energy for more important matters.
- The Chocobo is REALLY deep inside here -- all the way to the "Old Bypass" area. How it got up and down all of those ladders is beyond me. You have a battle to win, but fortunately you don't have to hoof it all the way out again.

- When prompted to do so, return to the surface to check in with Sam and put this one in the "W" column.

##### Quest: Malicious Goons

Since we're in Wall Market anyway, let's take on this Quest next. Make sure that you have plenty of Phoenix Downs for the upcoming battle because the enemy has an Instant Death attack.

- Head north and talk to Madame M. Go just a little north to Sam's Delivery Service and ride it (for FREE now!) to Sector 5 Undercity -- Entrance. (Am I the only one who has trouble correlating the Delivery Service Map with the in-game Map?)
- Head north then west towards Aerith's house. Head to the green marker and fight a Tonberry.

This completes the Quest. Let's collect some items before taking on another one.

- On your way back into town, stop off at the Kids' Hideout and talk to Moggie. He will sell you Barret's Steel Pincers for 7 Moogle Medals. While you're here, buy the Moogle's Mortar. You're going to need that for another Quest.

I suggest equipping these weapons for easy battles long enough to learn their abilities and then set them aside so that Barret can equip weapons that are better suited to his particular skill set.

- If you have completed Reports, you can check in with Chadley. At this point, you should have nearly completed all reports except 15 (which I advised you to postpone), 18 (which takes a LONG time), and 20 (which isn't offered yet). If any of the other Reports are still incomplete, make a mental note of what still needs to be done and try to work towards completing them while we're still in Chapter 14.
- Head north a bit and take a Chocobo ride to the Wall Market -- Urban Advancement District stop. As you head North, you'll hear music. Talk to the NPC and he'll give you **Music Disc #24** which, hopefully, will soothe Barret and make him more mellow. Right.
- Continue North to the Weapons Shop. Here you can buy a Mythril Saber for Cloud. Equip it, etc., etc., etc.

- Go into the lobby of the Colosseum and talk to the Souvenir Shop Owner. He will sell you **Music Disc #12**.

##### Quest: The Power of Music

- Go to the jukebox that is in the fenced area just off the narrow N-S alley to the West of the Inn and talk to Betty. She wants you to find 3 Music Discs. One of them is #12, which we picked up on the way here.
- Go into the nearby Inn and talk to the gentleman in the lobby with the Tom Selleck moustache. He will give you **Music Disc #16**.
- Head up to the Honeybee Inn. Enter the alley that goes West from there and pick up **Music Disc #30**.

- Since we're at the North end of town anyway, head East and then North past Madame M's to the circular area by the lake. Talk to the woman with the high hair to get **Music Disc #7**, for a total of 28 out of 31.

- Go back to where Betty is and interact with the jukebox to play the Discs one at a time. In case you forgot which ones they are, they are highlighted for you with green markers.
- Completing this ridiculously easy Quest earns you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. III Manuscript.

##### Quest: Tomboy Bandit

- Go to Sam's and ride a Chocobo carriage to the Sector 5 Undercity Station. You'll see Cloud's favorite Bro Johnny nearby. He'll send you off to the Church. You can catch another ride up there.
- The obnoxious reporter Damon may intercept you on your way into the Church and give you another Quest. This is because his Quest is tied to your current one. If he does, just let him yammer on before going about your business.
- Grab the glowing Medicinal Flowers on the ground (you'll need them for another Quest) and talk to the ever-charming Kyrie. She'll send you to the Colosseum.
- Before leaving the Church, remember to pick up the **Chakra Materia** that we left behind in the next room earlier. (Potentially interesting side note: When we were here in Chapter 8, this Materia was glowing with a green color. Now it's glowing yellow. OK -- maybe not so interesting . . .)
- Hitch a ride to either Wall Market destination and go to the Colosseum.
- Stop by the room downstairs with the Bench and check your Inventory of Materia. You should have one orb of **Revival Materia**. If you do, buy two more from the nearby vending machine. Those will help with the upcoming battle.
- Now head over to the Gatekeeper and accept the "Special Match".

- Teleport back to Kyrie when prompted.
- After the scene at the Church, hitch a ride back to the Station to give Johnny his wallet back. Even though this Quest is already complete, you have to do this to get your "The Johnny Experience" Trophy later. Besides -- it's just the right thing to do.

##### Colosseum Challenges
