Believe it or not, this seems to be your best option.<br>- If you continue N, you can go ALL the way back to the source labeled as "3" in the Map above from Chapter 8. The road to the other 2 areas in the Map is now blocked, so you can't access those. Still, this is a lot less further from Wall Market than the place in the Collapsed Expressway, so there is little reason to go here.<br>Overall, you want to keep an eye on your MP in this Chapter because there don't seem to be any convenient places to refill them.<br>If any reader finds a better source of MP in this Chapter, I would appreciate it if you would share that with me. |

For Cloud's last dress and to earn the Best in the Business Trophy, we need to make decisions that favor Madame M's Questline, which we have not yet followed. Those choices, as usual, appear below in **bold red**.

- When Sam asks what Tifa looks like, choose the third response, " **She's great at handling the books**" (+1 for Madame M's Questline).
- Talk to the Barker in front of the Inn. When prompted, ask him "**How Much?**" (+1 for Madame M's Questline).
- Don't follow Johnny when he races past. Head directly from the Inn to Corneo's.

- Talk to Leslie at Corneo's and then head over to Sam's.
- When Sam flips the coin, choose the third reply, "**No deal**" (+0.5 for Sam's Questline).
- Head to the Honeybee Inn and then over to Madam M's as before. This time when she asks which hand massage Cloud wants, choose the first option, "**Luxury Course-3,000 gil**" (+2 for Madame M's Questline).
- Afterwards, Cloud will be asked his opinion of Aerith's outfit. Have him choose the last response, "**It matter what I think?**" (+1 for Madame M's Questline).
- Head over to the Colosseum. Do not interact with the small bottle in Johnny's room. Then win your 4 matches.
- You are going to have to beat Hell House in Hard Mode, and that is an adventure in and of itself.
  - In addition to the usual Hard Mode enhancements, Hell House now can spit out Tonberries and Mechs for you to fight, as if you didn't have your hands full with an enemy that is unreachable and virtually immune to damage.
  - As before, Hell House can and should be Poisoned so that it will bleed HP at those times when it is otherwise difficult to do any damage to it.
  - Hell House is susceptible to Stop, but I'm pretty sure that it will only work once per battle.
  - Another new asset you have is Bahamut, whose attacks can hit Hell House even when he's flying around out of reach. It costs 2 ATB bars to summon him, but it's probably worth it.
- Cloud still has a couple of major battles to fight after this, so try to leave him with 80 - 100 MP so that he can finish the Chapter comfortably. Aerith should not need to use any more MP in this Chapter, so feel free to use her MP liberally.

Defeating Hell House in Hard Mode will earn you Aerith's Telluric Scriptures IX Manuscript.

- Return to Madame M's, and she will give you a list of tasks to complete while Aerith gets her makeover. This is because your choices assigned 5 points to her Questline and only 0.5 points to Sam's. Two Quests will show on your Map now.
- Madame M claims that you won't be able to leave town, but apparently that doesn't include the Collapsed Expressway because you can still go there to top up your MP if necessary.
- When you're ready to proceed, head over to the gym.

##### Quest: Burning Thighs

- Do the Burning Thighs Quest again, then head to the marker and talk to Mireille (whose surname, incidentally, is "Dudley.") to initiate the next Quest.

##### Quest: The Price of Thievery

- Follow the marker and eliminate the hilariously not-too-bright thugs. A blast or two of Firaga will very nearly eliminate the pack.
- Completing this Side Quest in Hard Mode will earn you The Art of Swordplay Vol. VIII for your trouble.

##### Quest: Shears' Counterattack

- Follow the new marker to Madame M's, where the Gatekeeper is waiting for you. He offers you the Shears' Counterattack Quest, which is back at the Colosseum.
- Go to the Colosseum basement, choose the Special Match and take on the Jury-Rigged Cutter.
  - Equip Cloud's sword with a **Lightning**-**Elemental** Materia pair as well as **First Strike** and **Time** Materia.
  - Jury-Rigged Cutter is ridiculously fast and merciless, but it is susceptible to Stop.
  - You will have few opportunities to Heal, so casting Regen when the Cutter is Stopped makes a lot of sense.
  - Cutter's Magic Resistance is very high, but his Defense against physical attacks is relatively low, so prioritize your sword over magic.
  - As is the case with most super-fast enemies in this game, you probably do not want to trade blows with Cutter. [The Punisher Mode - Parry strategy](#chapter-4----mad-dash) we used against Roche and Reno works well against Cutter's melee attacks.
  - Cloud's Counterstance is also very effective since it is easy to anticipate Cutter's relentless attacks.
  - Defeating the Cutter in Hard Mode will earn you The Art of Swordplay Vol. IX.

You have now completed all of the Side Quests, so the Best in the Business Trophy should pop for you.

- Madame M awaits you after the fight and tells you to come back to her place.
- As before, Johnny will intercept you on the way inside and drag you to Corneo's. Talk to Leslie, then head back North.
- When you encounter Aerith, she should be wearing a pale pink dress with puffy sleeves and no necklace.

I can't believe how everyone in the game is so negative about this dress of Aerith's. I mean, it doesn't have the pizzazz of her red dress, but IMHO, suggesting that it attracts rodents and scares small animals is going a bit too far.

- Go to the Honeybee Inn and do the dance scene as before.

Cloud should emerge wearing the dress pictured below. It should have a huge black bow on the back and a red rose in the top center of the front, and Cloud should be wearing a tiara.

After Cloud exits the Honeybee Inn, the Dressed to the Nines Trophy should finally pop for you!

- Now finish the Chapter as before so that you get credit for completing Chapter 9 in Hard Mode.

Now it's time for us to go back and complete the other 15 Chapters in Hard Mode and earn that Hardened Veteran Trophy.

## Hard Mode--Chapters 1, 2, and 4

#### Chapter 1--Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Aside from the final battle against Scorpion Sentinel, this Chapter isn't much harder this time through.<br>- I found no less than 13 Mako Shards in this Chapter, of which you'll probably only need a couple. However, that reality isn't going to stop me from pointing out all of their locations to you. I'm on a mission here, so don't try to stop me. However, don't feel compelled to harvest all of the Mako Shards just because they are there.<br>- The good news is that you should enter the boss fight with full MP and should have no problem completing this Chapter on Hard difficulty. |

- After climbing the first flight of stairs, head E onto the balcony, then go S to smash some Shinra crates for your very first **Mako Shard** (1).
- As you head E towards the elevator, look straight ahead for a pile of 5 crates, one of which contains another **Mako Shard** (2).
- Ride the elevator up, then go down a ramp. Just W of the ramp are some more crates and another **Mako Shard** (3).
- Jessie will open a door that leads to an elevator. Look left (W) while facing the elevator, and you'll see another stack of crates, one of which contains another **Mako Shard** (4). What an embarrassment of riches!

- After passing through the 3rd set of lasers, look left for another stack of crates and another **Mako Shard** (5).

- After making it through the last set of lasers, look East for more crates and another **Mako Shard** (6).

- You'll descend a long ladder and land in the Reactor Core Upper Level-Right Bridge area. Due N of where you land is another **Mako Shard** (7).
- When you descend the next short flight of stairs, you'll see more crates, one of which contains another **Mako Shard** (8).

- A ways further you'll come to your next ladder down, but if you look straight ahead (W) you'll see some Monodrives floating around near some more crates. Among those crates is another **Mako Shard** (9), but you probably have full MP at this point, and retrieving the shard necessitates defeating the Monodrives. My advice is to just avoid this area altogether and head straight down the ladder.

- Dispatch the much-more-difficult-than-last-time Scorpion Sentinel.
  - You can basically follow the strategy outlined [earlier](#chapter-1----the-destruction-of-mako-reactor-1). You probably won't Stagger and Pressure him as easily as before, but the general flow of events shouldn't change much.
  - This time through, you can equip the Elemental-Lightning Materia pair on both Cloud's weapon and Barret's weapon to give your basic attacks some extra punch.
  - You also have lots more MP now, so you can throw spells more liberally. Remember that this boss has high Defense (326) but relatively low Magic Resistance (268).
  - Winning this battle in Hard Mode earns you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. IV Manuscript.
- After the battle, if you need some MP, turn right after going up that first ladder. The Mako Shard at (9) has regenerated and the Monodrives are now gone.
- Climb another ladder and shimmy left to save Jessie. You'll descend another ladder, and there is a crate containing a **Mako Shard** (10) right where you land.

- After you reunite with Barret, you'll be accosted by another Sweeper in the Armory. Fight it or just run from it, but in the SE corner of the area is another **Mako Shard** (11) if you want it. In fact, it's there even if you don't want it.

- In the SE part of the Utility Access area on B3, you'll fight some Monodrives. Nearby, the **Mako Shard** at (6) may have regenerated in case you need it, but you probably don't.
- Climb the next set of stairs, and on B2 at the 2 locations shown below, you'll find 2 more **Mako Shards** (12) and (13) that you probably still don't need.

- Take the elevator up and get the heck out before the reactor blows! Oh, and nice work completing Chapter 1 on Hard difficulty.

* * *

#### Chapter 2--Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Aside from the fact that Cloud is solo throughout this Chapter, this is much easier than Chapter 1.<br>- For the final battle, you just need to defeat The Huntsman, which will take about a minute.<br>- Make use of Cloud's AOE attacks (hold Square) and his Triple Slash to dispense with mobs of enemies quickly.<br>- Most enemies are human, so equip the **Fire**-**Elemental** Materia pair on Cloud's weapon. |

I didn't find any places to heal HP in the Chapter, but then again I didn't need any. There are more than enough Mako Shards for you to use your MP for healing and for attacking.

Remember that enemies with shields can usually be dispensed immediately with a low-level Fire spell.

Here is a quick summary of where to go and what to find:

- Follow the team until they split up, then head N. Head towards the marker on you Map. Then follow the "Is-he-real?" person for a scene.
- Head the only way that you can -- W down the alley, up stairs and down to get past the wreckage and on forward for a scene with Aerith.
- If you go to the dead end alley at the NW end of Loveless Street, you'll find a crate containing a **Mako Shard** (1).
- The branch of Loveless Street that dead ends to the E (2) has nothing that you need, so you can skip a battle by just avoiding the area.
- After clearing out the enemies around the fountain, you can find a **Mako Shard** (3) in the SE portion of the area and another **Mako Shard** (4) in the SW portion.

- Head to the rooftops -- up 3 ladders then down 3 more -- ending up in the Residential Area.
- When the street bends from going SE to going due S, there is a dead-end branch to the E with a battle and a crate containing a **Mako Shard** (5).
- When the street turns from going W to going S, look to the W for a crate containing a **Mako Shard** (6).
- A little further S, there is a dead-end branch to the E which holds another **Mako Shard** (7) for you.
- Returning to the main street, as you go S you'll see one last pile of crates to the right. One of them contains a **Mako Shard** (8) which should be more than enough to fill up your MP and see you through this Chapter.

- When The Huntsman shows up, hit him with Firaga immediately. This should Pressure him and take out several nearby foes as well. Follow up with a Focused Strike, and this "boss" should be very nearly Staggered.
- Defeating The Huntsman will earn you Cloud's Art of Swordplay, Vol. IV Manuscript.

* * *

#### Chapter 4 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Again equip the **Fire**-**Elemental** Materia pair on Cloud's weapon for those human enemies, but have **Lightning Materia** equipped for the battle against a couple of very durable mechs.<br>- You'll be guarding a lot in the boss battle, so the **Steadfast Block Materia** is also a good choice, since it will help restore ATB while guarding.<br>- Tifa will be joining the team at the end of the Chapter, so you should go ahead and equip her now. |

- You can -- and IMHO, should -- skip the motorcycle race at the beginning of this Chapter.
- If there are any ways to replenish HP in this Chapter, I didn't find them. Most of the battles are clustered together in the Sector 7-6 Annex. There are some Shinra Crates in there, and a couple of them contain **Mako Shards.** However, it is very difficult to locate and intentionally smash these crates during battle because the camera angles are just awful and sometimes it's very difficult to see where you're going.
- The good news is that you should be able to survive this Chapter with your initial store of MP. Strategic use of those AOE attacks and Triple Slash will even the odds without using any Magic when you get outnumbered. You can afford to use some Thunder and Thundara spells against the mechs, and you will probably need to heal a time or two in the battles leading up to the boss fight. That will still leave you plenty of MP for the battle against Roche.
- A strategy for defeating Roche is outlined in [the previous Section on Chapter 4](#chapter-4----mad-dash). This time, there is really no need to Summon.
- Roche's Pressured state is very brief, so you will be lucky if you can hit him with Focused Strike while he is in this state. However, Cloud's Parrys and Fire spells will get him Staggered quickly enough.
- You gain Cloud's Art of Swordplay, Vol. VI for defeating Roche.
- After Cloud returns to Stargazer Heights and talks to Tifa, remember to watch the lower left corner of the screen for the cue to press the Square button and equip Cloud and Tifa for the upcoming battle.
  - If you miss it, you can Retry at the beginning of the battle and get the cue again.
  - Equip both team members with **Healing Materia** and **Fire Materia**.
- The final battle of the Chapter is against a bajillion Mysterious Spectres and the Enigmatic Spectre. The battle proceeds a bit differently than it did in Normal Mode.
  - Start by defeating a Mysterious Spectre. This seems to be necessary in order to damage the Enigmatic Spectre.
  - You must Stagger the Enigmatic Spectre in order to defeat it, and Magic (even first level spells like Fire) will do much more to increase the Stagger Gauge than physical attacks.
  - So, you're going to need to build ATB by fighting Mysterious Spectres, then using that ATB to hit the Enigmatic Spectre with Fire. Don't waste your MP on Fira. Fire will do just fine. Repeat until you've Staggered the Enigmatic Spectre.
  - Once the Enigmatic Spectre is Staggered, beat it into submission.
  - Tifa starts with full MP and Cloud should have plenty left over for casting Fire. Fortunately, you shouldn't take a lot of damage during this battle, so Tifa should have plenty of MP to do for the minimal healing you will need.

- Defeating the Enigmatic Spectre will earn Tifa her Way of the Fist, Vol. V Manuscript.

## Hard Mode--Chapters 5, 6, and 7

#### Chapter 5 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Again, I'll give you a basic outline of the steps to take, emphasizing the locations of Mako Shards so that you can keep your MP topped up.<br>- Even in Hard Mode, this Chapter is relatively easy until you get to the final boss battle. This Chapter contains an abundance of Mako Shards so that you should be able to enter the final encounter with your HP and MP completely full, giving you ample resources to complete this Chapter at Hard difficulty. |

- After the opening scenes on the train, Cloud and Tifa fight a battle or two. Afterwards, they can heal at the **Bench** (1).
- Run across the tracks and N a bit, and you'll find a crate containing a **Mako Shard** (2).
- After a battle, Tifa will spot Barret. Use the stairs to cross over to where he is and fight another battle.
- After getting your bearings, proceed SW and enter the room on your right. In there, you'll find some crates, one of which contains a **Mako Shard**(3).
- You'll fight a couple more battles. In the area where you fight the second one, one of the crates contains another **Mako Shard** (4).
- Enter the small room on your right and smash the crates in the corner for a **Mako Shard** (5).
- As you exit the room, look right in the middle in between the tracks for another pile of crates and another **Mako Shard** (6).

- Keep following the tracks N and W until Barret veers left and up some stairs. This would be a good time to equip some **Ice Materia** for the many creatures that lie ahead.
- Keep following the linear path and eliminating enemies until you get back down on the area shown below.
  - On your left, you'll see a line of 4 crates, one of which has a **Mako Shard** (7).
  - Ahead and on your right you'll see a **Bench** (8).
- Go upstairs to cross over to the other side and fight some human enemies. Feel free to use some Fire magic on them, because the room contains 2 **Mako Shards** \-\- one in a stack of 4 crates behind the stack of pipes (9) and one in a row of 4 crates in the NW corner of this room (10).
- Follow Barret to the platform with the map. There you'll find a row of 4 crates, one of which contains another **Mako Shard** (11).

- Follow the tracks and hide behind a container before battling a couple of humans and a pair of Sentry Launchers. Take out the Sentry Launchers first with Thundara or Thundaga.
- As you head up the stairs, remember that the room straight ahead (W) contains a **Bench** (12) and the room up the stairs and to the right (N) has a battle en route to the boss fight. You can return to the Bench to heal after the fight if you'd like.
- When you go down the ladder into the Former Raid Yard area, look straight ahead. You will see 2 piles of crates across the way. The pile on your left (W) contains a **Mako Shard** (13) and you CAN collect that -- multiple times if necessary -- before the boss fight.

As far as the boss fight goes, follow the basic strategy laid out [here](#chapter-5----dogged-pursuit). Take out the various parts as they become available as targets -- Legs, Generator, and Pilot. You should have plenty of MP, so don't be shy about using your Thunder spells. Doing so will Pressure Crab Warden. Follow up with your Focused attacks, especially Tifa's Focused Strike since it recharges ATB and can be used several times in quick succession. In the end, this didn't really seem to be much harder than beating this boss at Normal difficulty, but maybe I've just learned more about the game since then.

Winning this battle will earn you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. V.

On to Chapter 6!

* * *

#### Chapter 6 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Chapter 6 doesn't even have an actual boss fight. You can earn this Chapter's Manuscript if you win a fairly easy battle within a pretty generous time limit.<br>- This is arguably one of the easiest Chapters to complete in Hard Mode. Benches should provide most of the healing that you'll need, and you can use your MP to easily take down mechs that are resistant to melee attacks.<br>- The directions below are going to be pretty sketchy, even for me. If you want more detail, just refer to the [previous section on Chapter 6](#chapter-6----light-the-way). |

- Kill a light to make the door open. Fight a couple of Gashtrikes and head E and N into the area with the disabled security mechs. At the N end of the room is your first **Mako Shard** (1).

- Continue E, down the stairs, then S, W, and N up the stairs. As indicated below, there is a **Mako Shard** (2) in the square region to the West, but you probably don't need it and you have to fight a battle to get to it, so it's best to pretend that it isn't there.

- Get your bearings, make your way to the fans and past them, and head over to the cargo platform. Rest at the nearby **Bench** (3) but I'd be inclined to leave the **Mako Shard** (4) alone for now. You'll be back here later, and you probably have full MP now and don't need it. Think of it as a savings account.

- Head E then N and up the stairs to defeat 3 Gashtrikes. Head N to douse the first lamp.
- Have Barret take out some Sentry Rays, then move the platform so that you can proceed S.
- Going E, N, and E again will get you to the second lamp you need to extinguish.
- Go down the conveniently-located ladder, ride a platform W, defeat some mechs, head S, and ride a different platform E.
- You can skip the side trip you took on your first pass through here that yielded 2 orbs of Materia. I have indicated that **in red** back in the [original section on Chapter 6](#chapter-6----light-the-way).
- Head S, then counterclockwise around the C-shaped area, continuing E and S to another control panel.
- Use the control panel to move the platform down and left. Now you can proceed E to put out the last lamp.
- Return to the cargo platform. That's W across the movable platform, clockwise around the C-shaped area, N to ride a platform to the W, and W to the cargo platform.
- Now use the **Bench** at (3) to recover HP and the **Mako Shard** at (4) to replenish some MP.
- Ride the lift to the west and climb the ladder up into the Plate Ventilation Facilities - Control Room. There is a **Bench** (5) up there, but I can't imagine why you would need since you just used one.
- Win the battle as before, which should take about 30 seconds. Go north to interact with the control panel and enter the Plate Ventilation Facilities - Interior, which is where you found the **Chocobo & Moogle Materia** in Normal Mode. This time through you will find Tifa's Way of the Fist, Vol. VI Manuscript.
- Don't miss the **Mako Shard** in the crates to the E of the control panel.
- Return to the cargo platform and ride it to the S. The next battle will begin as soon as you cross that bridge to the W, so before you do so, pause for a moment.
- At the base of the column just to your left in the next area, you'll see a stack of crates. One of them (circled below) contains a **Mako Shard**. If you need those MP, run directly to those crates and smash them. Here is a rundown on the battle itself:

- You'll be fighting 6 mechs:
  - Sentry Ray x 2
  - Sentry Launcher x 2
  - Sentry Gun Prototype x 2

- The Sentry Rays and Sentry Gun Prototypes are out of reach for melee attacks. Have Barret unload his Overcharge on one Sentry Ray and follow with Maximum Fury on the other. If he has Lightning Materia linked to his weapon, this should take out both of them.
- One good Thundaga will finish off a Sentry Gun Prototype and will Stagger a Sentry Launcher. You can finish off the Sentry Launchers with physical attacks.

Climb some ladders to meet up with Biggs and end this ridiculously easy Chapter.

* * *

#### Chapter 7 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Resources are going to be a bit tighter in the Chapter, especially since there's a pretty hairy boss fight at the end.<br>- Navigating can be tricky. I'll walk you through the basics, but you can always look back to [the original section on this Chapter](#chapter-7----a-trap-is-sprung) if you need help finding your way. The maps of the mako shard locations are inserted there for your convenience. |

- Follow Tifa's creative solution for getting to the ground and head down towards the reactor.
- After descending a ladder and heading S in the area shown below, you'll find a stack of 3 crates, one of which contains a **Mako Shard** (1) that you probably don't need yet. Before breaking the crates, go down the short flight of stairs and fight a battle. Then heal and go back up the stairs to smash the crates to restore MP.

- Go down another ladder and advance a bit to fight 4 mechs. There is a **Mako Shard** (2) in the SE corner of the area. If you don't see it, you probably broke the crate while fighting the battle.

- After reaching the reactor core, the team will learn that they'll have to find a different exit route. Head through the doors to the N.
- Unlock the door marked "B8," wait with "b8ed" breath while it opens, fight a battle inside, grab the **keycard** from the W side of the room, and dispose of an M Unit. (It would be nice if you could use this card in a later room, but you can't leave this room without using it).
- Open the door to the N, go into the connecting passageway to fight a battle, and enter the "B7" door. Fight the battle, get the **keycard** from the W side of the room, dispose of the component of your choosing, and smash the crates by the Materia in the NW corner for a **Mako Shard** (3).

- Exit S for a battle in the connecting passageway. The crates don't contain anything useful, but be sure to grab the **keycard** from the SW corner.
- Enter the B6 door, fight some human enemies, get the **keycard** from the W side of the room, and dispose of 2 components.
- Exit N into the connecting passageway where -- surprise! -- no battle awaits you!
- Enter the B5 door, fight the battle, grab **keycards** from the E side of the room and the NW corner. Dispose of 2 components and smash the crates near the S end of the room for a **Mako Shard**(4).

- Exit S and head into the connecting passageway and defeat 2 Elite Shock Troopers. Continue climbing until you reach the Armory for another battle.
- Eventually, a Cutter will come out, opening the path forward. Destroy it and move on up.
- In the next room ("Utility Access") you'll find a **Mako Shard** (5) along the W wall. [Grab the shard repeatedly](#completing-chapters-in-hard-mode) until everyone has full MP, but you can probably hold off on healing since a Bench is forthcoming.

- Up and up we go, eventually arriving at the room with the annoying lever-pulling challenge. This time, you only need to do it once successfully to open the door to move forward. I don't see any reason in doing it 3 more times just to open the Waste Storage room. There are items inside, but you can't use them in battle, and you don't need the money you can get by selling them. I'd suggest sparing yourself the frustration and just moving on.
- Once the door to the N is opened, you'll find a **Bench** (6) where you can recharge and equip for the final battle of the Chapter.

For the fight against Airbuster, the basics can be found [here](#chapter-7----a-trap-is-sprung). You want the **Lightning**-**Elemental** pair on Barret for sure, since there will be many times in this battle when he will be the only one who can hit the enemy, which also means that he'll be the only one recovering ATB at a reasonable speed. I would definitely equip everyone with Revival Earrings again, but that's just my personal preference. Team members have a bad habit of dying when no one else has any ATB to revive them, and when that happens things can go south in a hurry. Otherwise, try to Pressure Airbuster with some Thunder-based spells when you can and then use Tifa to Stagger as she has done so many times before.

Beating Airbuster earns you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. VI.

## Hard Mode--Chapters 10, 11, and 12

#### Chapter 10 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- This Chapter is kind of the reverse of most Chapters because you fight the boss battle at the beginning instead of the end.<br>- After that, you have many opportunities to heal and restore MP before some tough battles at the end. |

- The Chapter begins with a boss fight against Abzu. He is weak against Fire, so equip Cloud and Tifa's weapons with the **Fire**-**Elemental** Materia combination. Abzu also has an attack that can wipe out your entire party, so I would advise having Revival Earrings on everyone.
- This is the toughest battle of the Chapter, so it's okay to invest some MP in winning this.
- [The earlier Section of the guide on Chapter 10](#chapter-10----rough-waters) give some tips for winning this battle.
- Defeating Abzu will earn you Tifa's Way of the Fist, Vol. VII Manuscript.

Since there are so many Mako Shards in this Chapter, I'll give some really basic navigating notes and insert maps so you don't miss anything.

- After beating Abzu and the first Sahagin, grab the **Mako Shard** (1) and then make your way to the **healing bench** (2).

- In the room where you turn on the power generator, you'll find a crate containing a **Mako Shard** (3).

- Drain the water from pool to the East and retrieve the Key to the Sewers so that you can move forward.
- After descending a ladder, you will need to head E to the next switch and the ladder down. The crates directly in front of you contain a **Mako Shard** (4).

- Soon after you cross the treacherous rafts, you'll approach a door. Just to the right is a crate with a **Mako Shard** (5).

- Soon afterwards, you'll pass through a room with a **Bench** (6).

- The dead end (7) shown above has another **Mako Shard**.
- When you exit the room containing Shard (7), you can head N or S. There is no reason to go S unless you REALLY need the 2 **Mako Shards** (8) to be found there and are willing to fight a couple of battles to get to them. My advice is to follow the path of least resistance by climbing the ladder and then going right.
- As you leave Aqueduct 1, you'll see some crates right before the path takes a 90 degree turn to the S. One of them contains a **Mako Shard** (9).
- On your way to the control room, you'll pass some crates right next to the pump that Tifa and Aerith use. One of them has a **Mako Shard** (10).
- When you climb down the ladder into this last area that you drained, you'll pass several crates. You can get **Mako Shards** from the first pile (11) and from a solo crate (12) on the path that crosses from W to E.
- The last battle is against 3 Sahagins. Use your Fire magic liberally, and this battle (and Chapter 10) will end quickly.

* * *

#### Chapter 11 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- I gave you a pretty detailed description of how to navigate this Chapter [when we went through in Normal Mode](#chapter-11----haunted), so look in that part of the guide if you get stuck.<br>- You want to enter both boss fights with full MP, but that shouldn't be too hard to accomplish if you make use of the many Mako Shards in the Chapter. |

Since there are so many Shards here, I'll outline for you where each fits into the flow of the Chapter and illustrate with maps.

- After you descend the second ladder, you will be drawn towards a door. After fighting the battle, claim the **Mako Shard** from the crates outside, just N of the door (1).
- After dropping down from a ladder into Switchyard 2 - Section B, you will be drawn into a fight. There is a crate containing a **Mako Shard** (2) next to a container along the E boundary. You will probably break this crate during the battle.

- Inside the Maintenance Facility, you'll go W, up some stairs, E, and then N through a door. You'll find the next **Mako Shard** (3) in that room.
- Continue E and slightly N to a room with exits to the N and and S. In here, you'll find a Ghost and a crate with a **Mako Shard** (4). You probably don't need it yet, and we're coming back through here after a Boss battle, so your best plan is probably just to leave it be for now.
- Continue N and down some stairs to the W. Follow the linear path to the **Bench** (5).

- Defeat the Ghoul. [The Section of this guide on Chapter 11](#chapter-11----haunted) has some tips in case you need a reminder. You earn Aerith's Telluric Scriptures, Vol. X for winning this battle.
- Retreat to heal at the previous **Bench** (5), pass through the room where you fought the Ghoul, go down 1 flight of stairs, and head S to the marker. Remember that you left a **Mako Shard** at location (4) above.
- Interact with the controls and make your way to the **Bench** (6) that is right before the exit to the outside.

- Interact with the turntable controls and fight several waves of enemies. Afterwards, you can backtrack to the last **Bench** (6) and then replenish some MP from the **Mako Shard** (7) shown in the map above (although you might have broken this crate during the last battle).
- Take the new path to the NW. The crates just to the left of the ladder contain a **Mako Shard** (8).
- When you get to the area with the 2 Drakes flying around, you can find another **Mako Shard** (9) in the southernmost tip of the area.
- Move one last engine and follow the new overhead path to the **Bench** (10) that allows you to heal before fighting Eligor.

- You earn Tifa's Way of the Fist, Vol. VIII for winning this battle.
- Move forward to experience the closing scenes with another Chapter completed on Hard difficulty.

* * *

#### Chapter 12 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Chapter 12 is a very straightforward, linear Chapter.<br>- Nearly all enemies are Fire-weak, so be sure to equip weapons with the **Elemental \+ Fire** pair.<br>- Notice that when Cloud is ascending solo, there is a Mako Shard on nearly every level that has enemies. Therefore, don't hesitate to throw a Fire spell at those flying enemies that you can't reach. |

Even though the path is pretty obvious (just keep going up) I think that I have some worthwhile tips to share about your ascent, so I'll give you a basic outline of the flow of the Chapter.

- After the opening scenes, you enter one of those annoying battles against hordes of Mysterious Spectres. In Hard Mode, you have to Stagger one of them to Pressure the Enigmatic Spectre. Good luck with that. I always seem to defeat them before I can fill their Stagger gauges. If you defeat enough of them, the battle will end even without emptying the Enigmatic Spectre's life gauge.
- Cloud will enter the Pillar alone and will immediately find a **Bench** on the ground floor where he can recover from the last battle. I'm not including a map because -- well, I mean, it's right there.
- Be sure to equip Cloud with the **Elemental \+ Fire** Materia pair and a **First Strike**. As you work your way up, keep in mind the power of Cloud's Triple Slash ability against multiple enemies.
- Win the battle on 2F, heal, then head to the NW corner to recharge MP with a **Mako Shard**(1).

- Up we go to 3F for another battle. This time the **Mako Shard** (2) is at the 7:00 position on the circular part of the area.

- On 4F you won't find any Shards or battles, but you will have a reunion with an old comrade. Take the ladder to the N to 5F.
- Just clockwise from where you arrive on 5F is a solo crate with a **Mako Shard** (3), but you might want to save it until after the battle. The Helitroopers that you fight can take a lot of health away before you can bring them down to earth, and you will probably want to throw some Fire spells at them. Remember that you can Pressure them by Guarding while in Punisher Mode to Parry their attacks.
- When you're done, go S and collect another **Mako Shard** (4) from the crates, then move on up to 6F.

- When you arrive at 6F, you'll be staring right at the crates that contain a **Mako Shard** (5). Again, you probably want to go N, win the battle, heal, and then return here to recharge MP instead of smashing the crates immediately.
- Proceed on up to -- you guessed it -- 7F.

- There is no battle on 7F, but don't go near the stairs up until you've snagged the **Mako Shard** (6) just N of them. If you start up the stairs, you'll be thrown into a scene and won't be able to collect this Shard later without taking an unacceptable amount of damage.

- The story shifts to Aerith's attempts to reach Seventh Heaven. Help her do so. There are no battles to fight until the action resumes at the Pillar.

- From here on, the Turks will be firing bullets at you any time you are not behind cover, so you no longer have the luxury of lingering in any area that is not protected. It is a good idea to check your map before leaving a protected area so that you can get there as directly as possible with minimum damage.
- You're headed for the ladder at the N end of 8F, but to get the **Mako Shard**(7) you have to follow a U-shaped path to the SW area. You might want to decide just how badly you want that Shard before proceeding. If you opt to get the Mako Shard, you're going to have to fight 2 Sentry Guns that you could otherwise avoid. You'll also suffer more damage from the Turks' gunfire. I'm inclined to leave this shard alone.
- Either way, the route to the ladder takes you past a pair of Slug-Rays that you'll have to defeat.

- On 9F, avoid the E side of the area, which only contains an unnecessary battle. Instead, follow the path along the W side shown below. There are 2 crates here ((8) and (9)) containing **Mako Shards**, so when fighting the 3 Slug-Rays, try to avoid breaking the crates. One Thunder spell will take out a Slug-Ray, so if both Tifa and Cloud go into battle with an ATB bar, minimal melee attacks should be necessary. You can then use the Shards to replenish the MP spent defeating the Slug-Rays.

- When you arrive at 10F, a crate with a **Mako Shard** (10) is staring you right in the face. Grab it.
- On your way to the next **Mako Shard** (11) on your right, you'll have to fight a pair of Elite Riot Troopers. Use Fire spells to knock them down and neutralize their shields, then use the Mako to replenish the MP you used.
- After defeating the Elite Riot Troopers, do a U-turn as shown below and vault over the obstacle to reach another crate with a **Mako Shard** (12). For those of you keeping count, that's a 30% refill on your MP on this floor! That's good, because this is your last chance to replenish MP before the upcoming boss battle.
- Endure Reno's taunts and follow Tifa on the rather gruesome trip up the ladder to 11F.

- You'll fight some more battles on 11F and 12F and have a scene on 13F before finally getting to rest at the **Bench** (13) on 14F. While you're there, be sure to equip Barret also since he will be participating in the upcoming fight.

- Your MP should be very nearly full when you face the bosses, in this case Reno and Rude.
- I outlined the basic strategy for this fight in [the earlier section on Chapter 12.](#chapter-12----fight-for-survival)
- A victory will earn you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. VII.

## Hard Mode--Chapters 13, 14, and 15

#### Chapter 13 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Chapter 13 follows a path that is essentially linear. You really don't have a lot of choices about your actions.<br>- Cloud can afford to use his MP in the first half of this Chapter since he isn't active in the second half. However, Barret and Tifa will need their MP for the final Boss battle, so try to conserve those.<br>- In preparation for that last half of the Chapter, equip Tifa and Barret each with 2x **MP Up Materia**.<br>- Remember the general rules about recovering HP and MP:<br>  - Do any necessary healing BEFORE smashing crates that contain Mako Shards.<br>  - Hold off on healing when you are nearing a Bench. |

- After finding Barret, the team makes the loooonnngggg run to Aerith's house.
- After the scenes there, take the SE exit from the Sector 5 Slums to the entrance to the collapsed expressway that Aerith and Cloud took at the beginning of Chapter 9.
- After passing the first mechanical arm, you'll climb a ladder that Aerith kicked down for you. Just to the left, there is s **Mako Shard** (0) in the [same location as in Chapter 9](#hard-mode--chapters-3-8-and-9).
- When the path splits, take the N path and look very closely along the N wall for a crate in the shadows that contains a **Mako Shard**(1).
- You'll fight 2 more battles and then go through a reverse-S shaped curve. Just past that, look for a crate with another **Mako Shard**(2).

#### Mako Shard Maps -- Chapter 13

- After going down a ladder, you'll be in the Bandits' Den. In the area where you fight the second battle with Bandits, there will be another **Mako Shard**(3). It's pretty likely that you'll break it during the battle, but that's okay. You get the MP anyway.

- Once you reach Evergreen Park, you'll find a **Bench** (4) where you can rest your weary bones after your arduous trek.

- Head into the Underground and follow Barret to the Sector 7 Slums. Continue to follow Barret and then Wedge's cat. Predictably, the team gets separated and Barret will be going solo for a while.
- Right where Barret first arrives in the Researcher Access area is a **Bench**(5) which he probably doesn't need at this point.
- As Barret winds through the S-shaped path, there are lots of Shinra crates on raised shelves Two of them (at locations (6) and (7) below) contain **Mako Shards.** You might want to save these for now and come back and get them after the next battle against some Bloodhounds.

- After shooting out a fan, Barret will climb a ladder and arrive at an area shaped like an inverted U. At the far right side of the crates on the shelf is another **Mako Shard** (8).

- After reuniting with Tifa, Barret will encounter a row of 3 fans. Behind the first one (to the NE) is a crate with a **Mako Shard** (9).

| **Planning Ahead**<br>**Mako Shard** (9) above is your last source of MP in this Chapter. Barret and Tifa still have a few battles ahead, and they're also going to need some MP for the upcoming Boss fight. Do everything that you can from now until then to conserve MP. Here are a couple of suggestions:<br>- When, for example, you see animal enemies ahead, equip both team members with an **Elemental**-**Ice** Materia pair on their weapons so that you do maximum damage in minimum time and thereby minimize healing.<br>- Avoid throwing Magic spells unnecessarily, especially Ice spells which agile enemies can dodge fairly easily.<br>- Give both Tifa and Barret **First Strike Materia** so that they have ATB for special attacks at the start of each battle.<br>I'm pretty sure that the enemies (and their weaknesses) that you face between here and the Boss are accurately documented in order below:<br>1. 2x Bloodhound (Ice)<br>2. 1x Cutter (Lightning) and 2x Monodrive (Wind)<br>3. 2x Varghidpolis (Ice) and 1x Bloodhound (Ice)<br>4. 16x Bugaboo (Wind) |

- After much battling and winding around, Tifa and Barret will arrive at a **Bench**. Use it to recover, and redistribute Materia as necessary for the upcoming boss fight.

- An outline for defeating the Failed Experiment is outlined in [the previous Section on Chapter 13](#chapter-13----a-broken-world). You might consider equipping Star Pendants to avoid Failed Experiment's Poisonous attacks.
- Defeating Failed Experiment will earn you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. VIII.

* * *

#### Chapter 14 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Chapter 14 is a bit more wide open than most Chapters because you are running around doing Side Quests.<br>- We're going to skip some Side Quests, but we want to be sure to complete the ones that lead to Manuscripts. In case you didn't notice, this Chapter has a whopping EIGHT Manuscripts that you can earn. Wowee!<br>- Places to recover MP will be few and far between until you go down in the sewers with Leslie. Really, this just means that if you feel that your MP are getting low, you're going to have to go to more trouble to refill them. The resources to complete the Chapter are available to you. |

When Cloud wakes up, he should be alone in a room with Wedge. Go outside, and you should get your third and final Resolution, this time with Barret. (Here's why. On our most recent pass through Chapter 3, we did just 2 Quests, giving 2 x 2 = 4 points to Tifa. In Chapter 8, we did 0 Quests, which gives 0 points to Aerith. Regardless of who earned 1 point at the beginning of Chapter 10, both Tifa and Aerith have fewer than 6 points, resulting in Barret's Resolution).

In a moment, I'll direct you to the Quests that you want to undertake, but first I want to point you to the places that you can recharge your HP and MP.

A number of places are available for restoring your HP, and your HP will be refilled each time you complete a Quest. If you need a refill in between, the Bench at the Undercity Station is a good choice for 2 reasons:

1. You can easily reach it via Chocobo coach.
2. It is close to the available Mako Shards.

Mako Shards can be found in the same 3 places as in Chapter 8. Those locations are shown in the map below.

#### Mako Shard Map -- Chapter 14

Now that you know where to heal, I recommend that you take on these Quests in the following order. The **blue** title of each Quest is also a hyperlink to the information about that Quest from our first trip through Chapter 14.

[Missing Children](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope)

- In [my earlier battle notes](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope), you can obviously ignore the content about learning an Enemy Skill, since you have already acquired it.
- There is a **Bench** in the area where you fight this battle, but it doesn't do you much good since your HP are restored after the fight anyway.
- Feel free to use Fire spells as necessary, as we will be passing 2 Mako Shards in just a moment.
- You will earn Tifa's Way of the Fist, Vol. X for defeating the Phantoms

[Chocobo Search](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope)

- Your path from the first Chocobo to the second one takes you right past **Mako Shards** 1) and 2) above. Use as necessary.
- Predictably enough Rust Drake is MUCH tougher this time around. Battle suggestions appear in the box below.

- After defeating Rust Drake, you can have Sam's Delivery Service take you to the Undercity Station and use the Bench there. Then take another ride to the Collapsed Expressway -- Entrance.
- On your way to the last Chocobo, you will pass by 2 **Mako Shards**. They are 1) and 2) on the map located [here](#hard-mode--chapters-13-14-and-15).
- You will get Way of the Fist, Vol. XI for beating the Trypapolis enemies. Strategy notes are [here](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope).
- After checking in with Sam, run by and talk to Madam M to start the next Quest. Then catch a ride to the Sector 5 Undercity - Entrance and head towards Aerith's house for your next Quest.

[Malicious Goons](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope)

- My original strategy notes are [here](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope), but our Materia is now developed to the point that Instant Death is no longer much of a threat.
  - Equip 2 members of your team with the **Warding** \- **Subversion** Materia combination in their armor, and they will be immune to Tonberry's instant death attacks.
  - You can only equip 2 members this way because you only have 2 **Warding Materia**. Just don't attack with your 3rd team member.
  - The Sharpshooter's Companion Vol. IX is yours for defeating the Tonberry.
- Backtrack to the nearest Chocobo station and grab a lift to the Undercity Station and let Johnny tell you his woes (and, I guess, his "Whoas").

| **Resetting Shinra Crates**<br>Fun fact -- Using the Chocobo service to ride from one location to another will reset Shinra Crates the same way that Saving and then reloading your game will. Since we used the Chocobo service since we last visited Sanctuary Way, those 2 crates that we smashed for Mako Shards on our way to fight Rust Drake have now reset, allowing you to collect them again if you so desire.<br>Teleporting after defeating an enemy -- like returning to Ms. Folia after defeating the Phantoms to complete the "Missing Children" Quest -- has the same effect. Whenever the game reloads, the crates seem to be reset. |

[Tomboy Bandit](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope)

- As noted above, if you walk (instead of ride) from Johnny to the Church, you can collect 2 **Mako Shards** from locations 1) and 2) [here](#hard-mode--chapters-13-14-and-15).
- If you do this, watch carefully as you approach the exit from the station and see if you can spot a familiar person walking past the mesh fence from left to right before you squeeze through the hole in the fence.

- Original strategy notes for winning the challenge are [here](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope). It will probably take 2 Blizzagas instead of one to Pressure Hellhound enough that you can Stagger him.
- Defeating the Beastmaster and Hellhound for Kyrie will earn you Cloud's Art of Swordplay, Vol. XI.
- You just used a bunch of MP to defeat Hellhound, so you probably want to collect those same 2 Mako Shards after your Colosseum battle. Just run back through Sanctuary Way on your way to returning Johnny's wallet. The Shards should be waiting for you.
- When you're ready to move on, catch a ride to Evergreen Park. You should begin the next quest with full HP and MP.

[Subterranean Menace](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope)

- You are basically following the same path that Barret and Tifa took getting to the Failed Experiment, except in the reverse order. The Mako Shards and the Bench that you pass should be in the same locations as before.
- There is a **Bench** in the Break Room right where you land when you go down the first ladder. In other words, it's located where it does you the least good. It is (10) in the maps in the section on Chapter 13 on this same page.
- After you pass through the broken fan, you can go left and collect a **Mako Shard** at location (9) shown on the map in the section on Chapter 13 above.
- In the B3 Researcher Access area, you can collect a **Mako Shard** at location (8) in the Chapter 13 section above. The crates are overhead and appear to be out of reach, but somehow Cloud can smash them anyway.
- Original strategy notes on the battle are [here](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope). However, since you now have more tools at your disposal, I'll offer some modifications:
  - The enemy is susceptible to Stop, which you now have at your disposal. It can result in a Counter Flare if Type-0 is not Staggered, but if cast when he IS Staggered, the Stagger gauge will not drain for the duration of the spell.
  - Let Barret do the bulk of the damage to the Upper and Lower Bodies since he can do so from a safe distance.
  - Once he is Staggered, cast Stop to freeze the Stagger gauge.
  - If you have the Gotterdammerung equipped, a Limit Break should easily crippled the Horns. If you think that's cheating, then use ATB attacks instead.
  - With the Horns gone, you can now use your MP to throw spells at the Behemoth from a safe distance, and he will go down MUCH faster.
- Defeat the Type-O Behemoth, and Barret gets the Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. X.

[Corneo's Secret Stash](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope)

- There is a Bench in Evergreen Park you can use to recover HP.
- From there, you might want to ride to the Church and work you way S so that you can abuse [those two piles of crates](#hard-mode--chapters-13-14-and-15) (1) and (2) with Mako Shards to recover MP.
- Next, visit the vault in the Steel Mountain area and open all of the containers there.
- Ride to the Collapsed Expressway entrance and raid the second vault.
- Then head over to Wall Street and head into the sewers with Leslie.

There are several Mako Shards to help you survive this Quest. I'll outline their locations and give you maps as we go.

- Soon after defeating your first Sahagin, you'll arrive at a **Bench** (1).

- After a couple of more battles, you'll be in Aqueduct 3, where you'll find a **Mako Shard** in the location shown below (2).

- You'll pass through the door that you unlocked in Chapter 10 and arrive in Sector 7-6 Main Aqueduct. Head to the crates N of where Leslie is leading you for a **Mako Shard** (3).

- Right after Leslie offers to take the lead, you'll reach a **Bench** (4).
- You'll go clockwise around a square area, eventually fighting 3 Sahagins. Just after that, look for a stack of crates with a **Mako Shard** (5).

- Your next battle is against an Abzu Shoat. There is a crate in the SW corner of that room with another **Mako Shard** (6).
- Your merry chase will lead you S into the Old Trunk Line area. After you fight a battle, go to the W portion of that area and smash a crate for a **Mako Shard** (7).

- Your showdown with the Mischievous Shoat takes place in the Former Disposal Area. Here you will find 2 **Mako Shards** \-\- one on the N side of the room (8) and one to the W (9).
- Be sure to pick up another pair of **Protective Boots** from the Chest (A) on the way back. You will want these when we return to Chapter 17.
- Follow Leslie to the ladder and throw a switch. Backtrack to Corneo's Vault and fight the Sahagin Prince. You will earn Tifa's Way of the Fist, Vol. XII for defeating him, and after the battle, you can claim a **Mako Shard** (10) from the N side of the room.

- Return to the ladder by the switch, climb up, and follow Leslie. When you descend a ladder into the S horizontal portion of Aqueduct 4, look around and you should find a crate with a **Mako Shard**(11). This is the same as location (5) in this same section. The Shard seems to regenerate.
- Leslie will lead you to a door and tell you to wait outside. Follow him anyway, and you'll find a **Bench** (12) (which is the same as **Bench** (4)) where you can recharge before the final battle. It is entirely possible to have full HP and MP at this point.

- Go outside and defeat Abzu and the Abzu Shoats.
  - A basic strategy for this battle can be found [here](#chapter-14----in-search-of-hope).
  - Remember that you have to run to a wall when Abzu is about to execute Blackwater Blast or Blackwater Deluge.
  - Fight the Abzu Shoats to gain some ATB, then use your ATB to throw Fira at the Horn until it is crippled. This will Pressure Abzu and allow you to Stagger him. You should have plenty of MP.
  - After the Stagger, the Shoats will drive you nuts if you leave them hanging around because they can and will disrupt your actions. Dedicate some time to eliminating them before focusing again on Abzu.
  - With the Shoats out of the way, the battle becomes much less frustrating, and you shouldn't have any trouble finishing off Abzu.
- You earn Tifa's Way of the Fist, Vol. IX for winning this battle.
- Afterwards, move towards the marker, and return to Marle when prompted to do so.
- You can then take a ride to the Wall Market Urban Advancement District and head over the wall to complete this Chapter.

Okay -- 4 more Chapters to go!

* * *

#### Chapter 15 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Chapter 15 has plenty of tough battles even before you get to the final boss fight. Even so, there should be ample Mako Shards to keep your MP topped up all the way to that ultimate battle.<br>- With a couple of exceptions, the path is ultra-linear, so you shouldn't have any trouble finding your way. I'll try to help you steer clear of areas that don't benefit your cause. |

- Remember that basically you are heading UP. If you look on the map for arrows pointing up, you can't go too far wrong.
- The second ladder takes you to Distillation Tower 2-3F. At about the 1:00 position you'll find some crates and a **Mako Shard** (1) to help you recover some MP after the first battle.
- You can skip Distillation Tower 1-3F (X). There is nothing there except a battle that you don't need to fight.

- After using your Grappling Guns for the first time, you'll be in a tunnel. To your left, you'll see some loose crates, one of which has a **Mako Shard** (2) for you.

- When you get to the Collapsed Service Tunnel area, you will fight several waves of enemies. There is a **Mako Shard** (3) kind of in the middle of the area, and you will more than likely break its crate during the battle. However, there is also a **Bench** (4) not far ahead, so you shouldn't need to heal after the battle.
- You can skip the area SE of the Bench (XX). There's a chest over there containing 3,000 gil that you don't need, and there is also a battle that you don't need to fight. The crates don't contain any Mako Shards, so there isn't any good reason for going over there.

- At 115m above ground level in Central Tower 9F, there is a set of stairs to the S and a place to grapple to the NW. The stairs take you directly to 10F. Grappling takes you through a more circuitous path past a Chest containing an Enchanted Ring.
- When you climb the ladder to 140m, you'll be in the Industrial Tank-4F area. Look to the SE for some crates, one of which has a **Mako Shard** (5).

- At 145m, after tiptoeing across a beam and fighting a Cerulean Drake, go to the small projection in the SW for another **Mako Shard** (6).

- At 155m you have to sidle around the Industrial Tank Scaffolding (or what is left of it). When you reach the end, you'll see some crates to smash for a **Mako Shard** (7).

- You will have to make your way from barrier to barrier while Valkyrie blasts you every step of the way. Be sure to adjust the camera so that you can see Valkyrie and not just his shots. It really helps reduce damage.
- After racing down the stairs, you'll arrive at Industrial Tank Control Wing-1F, where you get to fight 2 Blast-Rays. When you're finished with the battle, you can collect 2 **Mako Shards** \-\- one just SE of the SE pillar (8) and another just SE of the first one (9). Again, it is very possible that the cr8 at (8) will get broken during the battle.

- Up another ladder, you'll fight 2 Elite Heli Troopers. Yuk! Fortunately, you can heal at your pre-boss **Bench** (10) right after the battle.

- In the area where you fight Valkyrie, there is at least one crate containing a **Mako Shard**. I don't know whether you can intentionally break it or not, but it can definitely be broken by one of Valkyrie's many bullets.

- Back in [the previous section on Chapter 15](#chapter-15----the-day-midgar-stood-still), I outlined a strategy for defeating Valkyrie. Most of that still applies, but here are some additional battle suggestions for Hard Mode:
  - In Hard Mode, Valkyrie is no longer weak against Thunder. It is still weak to Wind, but Wind spells don't hit a mobile target like this very reliably. Still, you can use them on those rare occasions when Valkyrie is grounded and immobile, like after it uses Drill Dive.
  - Even so, equip Barret's weapon with the **Wind**-**Elemental** pairing so that his normal attacks will do enhanced damage.
  - Equip someone with the**Cleansing**- **Magnify** pair so that you can cast Resist during the second phase of the battle and avoid the Sleep and Poison status effects of Valkyrie's area effects.
  - Have Barret use Steelskin immediately and renew it when it expires. He is going to be your damage MVP, standing out in the open and exposed to danger, so you want to protect him as much as possible.
  - The Boss can be hit with Stop, so I would equip someone with **Time Materia**.
  - Remember that inflicting Stop on a Staggered enemy will freeze the Stagger gauge and give you more time to beat it up.
  - In the last stage of the battle, a drone will target a single character with a blue beam. One second after the beam locks on, an explosion will do massive damage. You can elude this by simply changing characters. The beam should follow the active character, so if you keep changing characters, your team should avoid nearly all explosions.
  - Defeating Valkyrie will win you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. XII -- and Barret will have totally earned it for his contributions to beating this tough enemy.

After a couple of scenes, we're on to Chapter 16.

## Hard Mode--Chapters 16, 17, and 18

#### Chapter 16 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- You're really going to need to watch your MP in this Chapter, because there are NO Mako Shards (that I found) and precious few Benches for restoring HP.<br>- You will have plenty of MP as long as you use them conservatively and spread out their use among your 3 team members. |

- You start with an extended sequence of battles against Guard Dogs and Shinra goons in the garage. Magic should only be used here for healing and for enemies with shields. For the enemies with shields, plain old Fire (as opposed to Fira or Firaga) will suffice. You just need to knock the enemies down so that you can hit them with normal attacks.
- Don't heal before entering the building. You will reach a Bench before facing any tough enemies. If you take the elevator, you will have to fight some easy battles in between floors, but as long as you have even one healthy team member you can win these. If someone gets KO'ed, they will be revived with 1 HP after the battle, and that's good enough.
- For navigating all of the running around you do inside the building, I refer you to [our first pass through Chapter 16.](#chapter-16----the-belly-of-the-beast)
- When you get to the Combat Simulator, there will be a **Bench** nearby. Use it to heal before fighting your challenge. Your HP will be restored afterwards.
- Next up is Specimen H0512.

- The notes that I gave you earlier on this fight can be found [here](#chapter-16----the-belly-of-the-beast), but his battle is a lot tougher in Hard Mode, so here are some suggestions:

  - You have one more 2-part battle after this one, so try to keep about 40 MP on reserve for each character for that encounter.
  - I would let Barret do most of the damage for you, since he can do it from a safe distance. He should always target the Left Claw, since crippling that will Pressure the boss. While Barret works on that, you can send Cloud and Tifa out to whittle down the H0512-OPT enemies and build some ATB.
  - Once the Left Claw is crippled and the boss is Pressured, hitting him with a couple of Firagas should bring him very close to Stagger.
  - For the record, the boss can be put to Sleep, and can be Stopped at least once.
  - You probably want to do something to combat Poison. One option is the **Cleansing** \- **Magnify** Materia pair for casting Resist. Star Pendant Accessories would be another option. If you keep your distance from the boss, you can get by without Revival Earrings.
  - At the beginning of the last Stage, Specimen will execute Mako Expulsion. You need to immediately move everyone to the left side of the screen to protect them from this.
  - Winning this battle in Hard Mode earns you Cloud's Art of Swordplay, Vol. XII Manuscript.
- Before getting on the elevator, look to the right and you will see a very welcome **Bench**. Use it to heal your wounds before getting on the elevator, because you have one more long battle to fight.
- Hojo is going to sic some pretty tough enemies on you. I gave you a rundown of these enemies earlier, so if you want a reminder of what you are facing and how to deal with them, look [here](#chapter-16----the-belly-of-the-beast).

Once this battle is over, you get several scenes before progressing into Chapter 17.

* * *

#### Chapter 17 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- As you may recall from our first time through, Chapter 17 is both lengthy and difficult, and this time through the enemies are definitely tougher.<br>- There are lots of Mako Shards, and I'll detail their locations for you again. Unfortunately, they are kind of clumped together, and you have to fight 3 consecutive boss fights with no chances to replenish MP.<br>- The good news is that you won't need many MP for 1 of those 3 boss fights, so you can kind of save them up for the last battle.<br>- Remember to keep adjusting your Materia and strategies to adapt to the enemies you will be fighting.<br>- If you find yourself ill-equipped for a battle, don't hesitate to hit the Options button and start again from right before that battle. Being properly equipped makes a HUGE difference in many of the upcoming battles because it actually seems to be the _raison d'etre_ of many of them. |

If the narrative for this Chapter sounds eerily familiar, that is because is pretty much a repeat of my narrative from our first trip through Chapter 17. I repeat it here for your convenience so that you don't have to keep jumping back to the previous section. You're welcome.

- Play through until you get to the Simulator and Chadley. Use the **Bench** (1) in the Employee Break Room to recover any HP lost in that first battle.

- Keep going until Cloud reaches the entrance to 1st Ward. He can rest at the **Bench** beside the entrance. In fact . . .

| **Chapter 17 Benches**<br>For the remainder of this Chapter, there will always be a **Bench** at the entrance to a Ward. You can and should use it before entering and, if possible, after fighting the first battle within that Ward.<br>With this in mind, I suggest that it is no longer necessary for me to insert Maps showing the locations of these Benches. I am confident that you can find them even without a picture showing you where they are. I'll include a reminder in the narrative and will assume that will suffice.<br>I will, however, continue to insert Maps showing the locations of other Benches that have less-than-obvious locations so that you do not miss them. |

- Before entering the 1st Ward, equip the armor on both Cloud and Barret with the **Lightning**-**Elemental** combo in preparation for the upcoming battle against a M.O.T.H. unit. This will cause the enemy's Lightning attacks to restore HP instead of doing massive damage.
  - M.O.T.H. Unit begins the battle surrounded by rotating blades which will prevent Cloud from getting in close for melee attacks, so don't even try. Barret's guns can still do damage.
  - Once the enemy sustains sufficient damage, it will drop those blades, allowing Cloud to beat it to death with his Punisher Mode.
  - Remember that you can steal an Iron Maiden from this enemy if you so desire.
- After defeating the M.O.T.H. unit, you should backtrack to the **Bench** outside the 1st Ward. There isn't another opportunity to heal for free before the next battle.
- Have Cloud and Barret follow the path to the PHS Terminal (com device), and then switch parties to Tifa and Aerith.

**Tifa and Aerith**

- They will go to the entrance to the 3rd Ward. A **Bench** is there, but they probably don't need it. Throw a switch and then use the PHS Terminal and switch back to Cloud and Barret.

**Cloud and Barret**

- Move the **Lightning**-**Elemental** combo for both Cloud and Barret from Armor to Weapon for the upcoming fight against a Blast-Ray.
  - When Staggered, Blast Rays put up a force field that prevents you from getting close to them.
  - You can still damage them with Barret's guns, Thunder-based spells, and Cloud's Blade Burst ability.
- Open the door to the 3rd Ward and switch teams.

**Tifa and Aerith**

- Tifa and Aerith will enter the 3rd Ward, open a door, and fight some Unknown Entities. Afterwards, they can backtrack to the **Bench** outside the entrance to heal without using MP.
- Next, the ladies will fight a bunch of Bloodhounds, so have **Ice Materia** on Tifa's gloves and somewhere on Aerith. After the fight, squeeze through the opening and smash the crates blocking the doorway for a **Mako Shard**(2).
- Defeat the Zenene, but before going up the stairs, smash the crates underneath them for a **Mako Shard**(3).

- Fight the 2 Zenenes. Head outside, go down the stairs, and head S to the Crates for a **Mako Shard**(4).

- Head N to fight some mechs.
- Climb the ladder, pull a switch, and head across the pods to another **Mako Shard** (5) and a PHS Terminal. Call the guys and switch parties.

**Cloud and Barret**

- Open the door to the 2nd Ward. Head over there. Save at the **Bench**, then go inside to fight 2 Zenenes.
- After the battle, as you head SW down a passage, you will find a pile of crates with THREE **Mako Shards**(6).
- Next up is another M.O.T.H. unit, so equip accordingly. After this battle, you will find 4 Mako Shards, so you don't need to skimp with your MP.
- In the NE-SW hall there is a **Mako Shard** (7) at the dead end to the NE and 3 more **Mako Shards** (8) at the SW end. Wow!

- Next up are 3 Sledgeworms. I still REALLY hate these things! There will be 4 more Mako Shards after this battle, so don't worry about throwing Thunder-based spells at those pests.
- Head up the stairs and go NE to the dead end room for a **Mako Shard**(9).

- Head up the stairs to Level 6 where you'll find another pile of Crates with 3 (!) **Mako Shards**(10).

- Cloud and Barret will fight a room full of Brain Pods. This time around, your Materia should be fully leveled, allowing you to ward off both Poison and Stop.
  - Brain Pods only have 2 strengths -- they will Poison anything in sight, and they can hit you with Stop.
  - Equipping Protective Boots will prevent being Stopped, and equipping the **Poison**- **Warding** pair on armor will prevent Poisoning.
  - If you put the **Elemental**- **Lightning** pair on your weapons, you can tear through these things very quickly while taking virtually no damage. (This is an example of my earlier point about proper equipment making all the difference).
  - Don't bother healing after this battle. You will reach a **Bench** before you have to fight again.
- Head upstairs to the entrance to the 4th Ward, heal at the **Bench**, and then go down the other side. On your way to a PHS Terminal, you get sidetracked by another mission. Call and switch parties.

**Tifa and Aerith**

- Turn around and smash any crates behind you for a **Mako Shard**(11). There may or may not be any. This is obviously the same as (5) above since the girls haven't moved, but in my game the crates were intact again. Maybe I reloaded the game since I last smashed these crates.

- Equip Lightning Materia and defeat the Sledgeworms. Then go down the ladder where a pair of Enhanced Shock Troopers awaits you. Hit them with Fire spells to Pressured them, then beat them down with physical attacks.
- Head over to Red and defeat all the Unknown Entities. The crate at the S end is one from which we already got a **Mako Shard**(4), but it may have regenerated for you.
- Take the S stairs to Level 4 and head N towards the elevator which, of course, doesn't work yet. Pull the switch and go over to the Central Control Terminal to open up the 4th Ward and call the guys to switch parties.

**Cloud and Barret**

- Go back up to the entrance to the 4th Ward. Rest at the **Bench** before heading inside.

- Boss #1 (Swordipede) is up next.
  - My strategy notes for this fight can be found [here](#chapter-17----deliverance-from-chaos).
  - When Swordipede is getting ready to fly off (around 50% health), remember to watch the lower left corner for the Menu/Square button icon so that you can shift Materia before the battle continues with the ladies.
  - Conserve Aerith's MP as much as possible. She is going to need them against Boss #4.
  - Defeating Swordipede in Hard Mode earns you Aerith's Telluric Scriptures, Vol. XII.

##### The Team Reunited

- After the battle, the band reunites. Take the elevator up and inspect the purple slime, then head to the new elevator.
- You will be asked if you want to go to the roof. You still have 3 boss battles to fight, and **this** **is your last chance to refill MP in this Chapter.** If you are worried about your MP levels, you can retreat to gather some Mako Shards fairly easily if you are willing to fight a couple of easy battles along the way. If you want to recharge MP, here's how:
  - You can only take 3 of your 4 characters on this side trip to refill MP. Barret is the obvious choice to leave behind. He only participates in 1 of the 3 upcoming battles, so it's okay if his MP aren't topped off.
  - Return to the elevator you rode to get here.
  - Choose to go to the Central Terminal Floor and follow the instructions below. There are 3 maps below to illustrate your path.
    - Head S across the pods and go up the stairs at the S end of Passage E.
    - At the top of the stairs, you'll have to fight 3 Unknown Entities. You can then claim a **Mako Shard**(4) right next to the stairs.
    - Head N and go up the first set of stairs in the middle of Passage E.
    - Head N and E and go towards the stairs that go up at the N end of 3rd Ward -- Feed Storage. You may have to fight a Zenene along the way, but there is a **Mako Shard** under those stairs.
    - Farm these 2 Mako Shards as necessary, then reverse your steps to return to the elevator. Take it to "The Drum - Top Floor." Interestingly, Barret isn't there waiting for you when you arrive where you left him.

- Up on the 70th Floor, there are 2 **Benches**. Use the first one you encounter (12) to restore HP before the next boss fight. If you pass it by, you'll get dragged into a scene.

- Boss #2 is Jenova Dreamweaver.
  - The battle is complicated, but I gave some basic tips [earlier](#chapter-17----deliverance-from-chaos).
  - You might want to equip everyone with Protective Boots to neutralize the boss' Stop attacks.
  - When Jenova has Shield in place, have Aerith throw down an Arcane Ward, but try to use Cloud and Tifa's Magic instead of Aerith's. Aerith is going to need as many MP as possible going into the final battle of the Chapter. Tifa, however, is done after this battle, and although Cloud has one more battle, his need for MP is minimal.
  - I found the battle to be lengthy but not particularly difficult. Try to focus on conserving Aerith's MP in anticipation of her next battle.
  - Winning this battle earns you Tifa's Way of the Fist, Vol. XIV.
- Retreat to one of the previous **Benches** before going up the ladder.

- Boss #3 is Rufus and Darkstar. Battle tips can be found [here](#chapter-17----deliverance-from-chaos), but since you now have the Stop spell (to which Rufus is vulnerable) we can modify our strategy slightly. I'll detail all of the steps here so that you don't have to go back and forth between pages.
  - Equip Cloud [as before](#chapter-17----deliverance-from-chaos), but **Poison Materia** is no longer necessary.
  - Start by having Cloud cast Haste on himself and then immediately use ATB Boost. If Cloud doesn't start the battle with at least 1 1/2 ATB bars, you might as well Retry, because ATB boost won't give you the full ATB bar you need.
  - Execute a Triple Slash, starting (and therefore ending) with Darkstar. This will break the link with Rufus and will immediately Pressure Darkstar, and his Pressured state lasts a good while.
  - Switch to Punisher Mode and beat up on Darkstar, filling your ATB gauge. Ignore Rufus, even if he attacks you. You want to have 2 full ATB bars when the next phase begins.
  - When the battle shifts to the next phase, execute another Triple Slash like before. Follow up by hitting Darkstar with Disorder. Besides doing some damage, this will shift Cloud to Punisher Mode and restore some of his ATB.
  - Beat up on Darkstar in Punisher Mode until he Staggers, and finish him off. Again, ignore Rufus. Try to have 2 full ATB bars when Darkstar goes down.
  - When the next phase begins, cast Sleep on Rufus.
  - Stand beside the napping Rufus while your ATB charges. As soon as you have 2 ATB bars, use one bar to hit Rufus with Braver, which will immediately Stagger him.
  - Now cast Stop on Rufus. This will freeze the Stagger gauge, allowing you plenty of time to beat up on Rufus in Punisher Mode before the Stagger gauge runs out. Nifty, huh?
  - Winning this seemingly impossible battle earns you Cloud's Art of Swordplay, Vol. XIV Manuscript.
- The focus will shift to Barret and Aerith. When they are in the elevator passing Wedge, watch the lower left corner of the screen for the Menu/Square button symbol. Push and hold Square so that you can access the Main Menu and swap out equipment before the last battle of the Chapter.

- Boss #4 is the Arsenal.
  - Battle tips can be found [here](#chapter-17----deliverance-from-chaos). For me, this strategy still worked really well in Hard Mode.
  - Remember to pay attention to defense. Keep Steelskin on Barret, and take cover when the Arsenal is about to launch a serious attack. You want to minimize the need for healing and resurrecting so that you can use your MP for offense.
  - Defeating the Arsenal earns you Aerith's Telluric Scriptures, Vol. XIII and leads you to the Chapter-ending scenes.

* * *

#### Chapter 18 -- Hard Mode

| **Important Points to Keep in Mind**<br>- Cloud's Punisher Mode Parry is your best weapon against the relentless attacks from the Whispers and Sephiroth. |

This Chapter begins with another Bike Mini-game. Mercifully, you can and should skip it.

My advice from our first trip through Chapter 18 appears [here](#chapter-18----destinys-crossroads), and I don't have anything to add for doing this in Hard Mode. The enemies are much stronger, but so is your team.

Defeating Whisper Harbinger will earn you Barret's Sharpshooter's Companion, Vol. XIV Manuscript.

Defeating Sephiroth will earn you Aerith's Telluric Scriptures Vol. XIV.

If you are as obsessed as I was about completing the Play Log and managed to Assess all 114 enemies, your Play Log should look like this (except you probably don't have all of the Manuscripts yet):

Completing all 18 Chapters in Hard Mode will earn you the Hardened Veteran Trophy and, assuming that you have already earned all of the other Trophies, you will be awarded the Platinum Master of Fate Trophy.

Aerith is here to congratulate you on your amazing accomplishment.

## Weapons and Abilities

Below is a list of each character's weapons, its starting and ending Attack and Magic stats, and any gains in HP, MP, Speed, and Luck that can be gained through upgrades. I included a column for the Reprieve ability because it can be an important consideration in some battles.

I didn't include Defense or Magic Defense because most of these increases are fairly minor and some of these increases are dependent on the character's current HP and therefore can change during the course of battle. Upgrades to these stats are reflected in the before-and-after pictures that can be found in the Section of this guide where each weapon was first acquired.

Empty cells represent Zero values. I felt that typing in those zeros would only detract from the readability of the table.

Please remember that statistics do not tell the whole story when it comes to choosing a weapon. Many upgrades do not show up in the basic numbers that you see when you equip a weapon. Many of these are "conditional" buffs, such as "Magic Damage Reduction 10% when Guarding." At some point, you should probably look at all the available upgrades for each weapon and see what it can and cannot do.

As far as abilities go, my purpose here is to give you a kind of "quick reference" to the Weapon Abilities of each character and their respective strengths and weaknesses. I omitted the descriptions of the abilities themselves since those are detailed elsewhere in this guide.

#### Cloud's Weapons

| Iron Blade | Triple Slash | 23 | 31 | 77 | 105 | 350 |  |  |  | Yes |
