#### Cloud's Abilities

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ability | Strengths | Weaknesses |
| Braver | - Can damage multiple enemies in an AOE.<br>- Can hit low-flying enemies that other abilities won't reach. | - No ATB regain.<br>- Won't hit high-fliers.<br>- Little Stagger increase. |
| Focused Thrust | - Cloud's best attack for increasing Stagger, especially on Pressured enemies.<br>- Attacks faster than Braver. | - Deals half the damage of Braver.<br>- Won't hit airborne enemies.<br>- No ATB regain. |
| Triple Slash | - Highly effective when faced with multiple enemies because attacks increase in strength.<br>- Good for clearing out swarms of enemies. | - Less effective when faced with a single enemy because attacks decrease in strength.<br>- No ATB regain. |
| Disorder | - Regains a significant amount of ATB.<br>- Eliminates time lag changing between Operator and Punisher Mode.<br>- Can cause significant Pressure when used in Operator Mode. | - Does less damage than Braver or Triple Slash. |
| Infinity's End | - Devastating attack that does substantial damage.<br>- Has more attack power when used on a Staggered enemy. | - Requires 2 ATB bars.<br>- Long charge time.<br>- Can easily miss an enemy that isn't Staggered. |
| Blade Burst | - Non-elemental Magic-based attack that can be launched safely from a distance.<br>- Requires no MP.<br>- Hits a wide AOE. | - Less effective when Cloud's build emphasizes physical damage. |
| Counterstance | - When attacked by an enemy, Cloud's damage is reduced by 80% and powerful AOE damage that increases Stagger is dealt. | - If Cloud is not attacked within a short time, his attack will be fairly weak.<br>- Ability needs to be initiated soon before an enemy attack for maximum benefit. |

#### Tifa's Abilities

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ability | Strengths | Weaknesses |
| Divekick | - Arguably Tifa's best ability for AOE damage.<br>- Combos well with her other regular attacks to reduce animation time. | - No ATB regain |
| Overpower | - Good at building Stagger and Pressure, especially when paired with her Triangle attacks. | - No ATB regain<br>- Deals less damage than Divekick or Starshower. |
| Focused Strike | - The best move in the game for raising the Stagger, especially of a Pressured enemy.<br>- Huge ATB regain.<br>- Difficult for enemies to interrupt. | - Deals less damage than many other abilities.<br>- Long charge and execution time. |
| Starshower | - Tifa's most powerful ability.<br>- Increases Stagger and Pressure.<br>- Increases the power of her next attack by 70%. | - No ATB regain<br>- Long charge time<br>- That's about it. This attack ROCKS! |
| Chi Trap | - Effective against enemies with low mobility and/or a weakness to Magic.<br>- Can hit multiple times. | - Reduced effectiveness if Tia is built for physical attacks rather than Magic.<br>- Trap is stationary and can be avoided by many enemies. |
| True Strike | - Increases a Staggered enemy's Stagger by 30% each time it is used.<br>- Deals high amount of physical damage.<br>- Combines nicely with Tifa's Triangle attacks. | - No ATB regain, but so what? |

#### Aerith's Abilities

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ability | Strengths | Weaknesses |
| Tempest | - If a fully charged Tempest connects, it can cause considerable damage and restore up to 80% of an ATB bar. | - The uncharged version is weak and takes time to charge.<br>- Mobile enemies can dodge the fully charged version. |
| Soul Drain | - Recovers MP, which are at a premium in Hard Mode. | - Does relatively little damage. |
| Arcane Ward | - One of Aerith's best abilities. It gets the most out of her MP and ATB.<br>- Works for all members of the team.<br>- Lasts quite a while (180 seconds). | - Character must stand inside the Ward, which can be hard to find or can be occupied by enemies. |
| Sorcerous Storm | - Can damage multiple enemies if they are close to Aerith.<br>- Does Magic damage without using MP. | - Aerith must be close to enemies to damage them, which runs counter to her usual attack-from-a-distance strategy. |
| Fleeting Familiar | - Damages enemies over a long period of time (240 seconds) even when normal attacks aren't possible.<br>- Augments Aerith's actions by supplementing damage inflicted. | - Only useful during long battles since short-term damage is minimal. |
| Ray of Judgment | - Aerith's strongest ability in terms of damage.<br>- One of a very few abilities that will increase Stagger percent. | - Requires 2 ATB bars.<br>- Significant startup time.<br>- Doesn't track enemies, so attack might miss. |
| Lustrous Shield | - Provides protection from projectiles.<br>- Provides some protection against enemies passing through. | - Shield is stationary and to be protected, the character must be standing behind it.<br>- Duration is relatively short (80 seconds). |
| ATB Ward | - Can help allies recharge ATB more quickly.<br>- Can be combined with an Arcane Ward to allow a character other than Aerith to quickly cast multiple spells. | - Costs 2 ATB bars.<br>- Doesn't actually increase ATB, it just redistributes it.<br>- To benefit from the Ward, a character must be standing in it, making it difficult to use with melee ATB abilities. |

#### Barret's Weapons

|     |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
|  |  | Starting | Final |  |
| Name | Ability | Atk | Mag | Atk | Mag | +HP | +MP | +Speed | +Luck | Reprieve? |
| Gatling Gun | Focused Shot | 19 | 19 | 77 | 77 | 950 | 21 |  |  | No |
| Light Machine Gun | Lifesaver | 22 | 33 | 62 | 93 | 350 |  |  |  | Yes |
| Big Bertha | Maximum Fury | 45 | 30 | 93 | 62 | 1250 |  |  |  | No |

#### Barret's Abilities

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ability | Strengths | Weaknesses |
| Steelskin | - Reduces damage by 30%.<br>- Greatly increases interrupt defense. | - Only lasts 90 seconds. |
| Focused Shot | - Greatly increases Stagger, especially on Pressured enemies.<br>- Damage increase is disproportionately greater as more ATB bars are consumed. | - Does less damage than Maximum Fury.<br>- Consumes All ATB. |
| Lifesaver | - Reduces damage to more vulnerable allies by diverting 70% of their damage to Barret.<br>- Cannot reduce Barret's health to below 1 HP. | - Damage isn't reduced. It is just redistributed. Therefore attention to healing is of paramount importance. |
| Maximum Fury | - Gets disproportionately stronger as more ATB bars are used.<br>- Inflicts significant damage, especially on Staggered enemies. | - Consumes all ATB.<br>- Doesn't increase Stagger. |
| Charging Uppercut | - Recharges Barret's Overcharge/Overrun gauge. | - Slow startup means that some enemies can evade.<br>- Barret must be close to his enemy, which is contrary to his normal ranged attacking pattern.<br>- Barret has more powerful abilities at his disposal. |
| Smackdown | - Deals strong AOE damage.<br>- Once initiated, it is difficult for enemies to interrupt. | - Slow startup gives enemies a chance to get out of AOE.<br>- Close-up combat just isn't Barret's style. |
| Point Blank | - Gets disproportionately stronger as more ATB bars are used.<br>- Has the most damage potential of Barret's melee attacks. | - Consumes all ATB.<br>- For close range only. Barret must be as close as possible to his target. |

## Important Revision Alert!

Somewhere around January 20, 2021 I submitted a revised version of this guide. You have been directed to this page because it is possible that this revision could hinder your efforts to obtain all of Cloud's dresses, thereby requiring you to repeat Chapter 9 if you want the Dressed to the Nines Trophy. I sincerely wish to avoid having you do that because unnecessary repetition runs counter to my purpose in writing this guide, and I do not want to disappoint or inconvenience my readers.

The following Q&A is intended to help you determine whether you are affected by these changes and if so what you need to do.

**Q:** How can I tell the "old" version from the "new" version?

**A:** Perhaps the easiest way to tell is that the new version includes pictures and descriptions of all the weapons, as well as the "Weapons and Abilities" section of the guide. The old version did not contain this information.

**Q:** Why is it important for me to distinguish between the 2 versions?

**A:** Mostly so that the following will make sense:

| **How to Tell Whether This Guide's Recent (January 2021) Revision Affects You**<br>The following readers will NOT at all be affected by this revision and can continue to follow the procedures in this guide without concern:<br>- Those who have ONLY used the new version in their current run.<br>- Those who started their run using the old version but have not yet started their second pass through Chapter 9 in the [Three More Dresses](#three-more-dresses) section of this guide.<br>However, if you STARTED your run using the old version and you have at least begun your second pass through Chapter 9 as described in the [Three More Dresses](#three-more-dresses) section of this guide, then you need to read on because some adjustments will probably be necessary. |

**Q:** I'm pretty sure that when I started using this guide, I was using the old version.

**A:** Well, that really isn't a question, but I'll tell you how to proceed anyway.

The only change that I made that will affect your playthrough is that in the 2nd and 3rd runs through Chapter 9, I interchanged the answers to the 4 questions that determine which dress Cloud gets after dancing with Andrea. The 2nd run is described in the section [Three More Dresses](#three-more-dresses) and the 3rd run is described in the section [Hard Mode--Chapters 3, 8, and 9](#hard-mode--chapters-3-8-and-9). In a nutshell then, all you need to do is to reverse the answers written in **red** between these 2 sections.

I should point out that **reversing these answers is also going to change which Side Quests are offered to you. Simply do all of the Chapter 9 Side Quests that are offered**. Instructions will appear in the section of this guide that contains the question answers you are using, as detailed in the next couple of paragraphs.

There are just 4 answers that are different between these 2 sections. In both situations, you ignore the barker and the innkeeper, and you do not follow Johnny. The 4 answers that are affected are:

1. How you describe Tifa to Sam when you first arrive at Wall Market.
2. What you call when Sam flips a coin after you visit Corneo's Mansion for the first time.
3. The hand massage course that you choose from Madame M.
4. How you describe Aerith's clothing to Madame M.

So, if, for example, you completed the [Three More Dresses](#three-more-dresses) section using the old version but have not yet begun the [Hard Mode--Chapters 3, 8, and 9](#hard-mode--chapters-3-8-and-9) section, then when you get to that latter section, use the responses to those 4 key questions that now appear in the [Three More Dresses](#three-more-dresses) section, since those are the ones that appeared in the [Hard Mode--Chapters 3, 8, and 9](#hard-mode--chapters-3-8-and-9) section of the old version.

If you started your 2nd run through Chapter 9 using the old version but, for example, never got as far as Madame M's, then when you get to questions 3 and 4 above, use the answers from [Hard Mode--Chapters 3, 8, and 9](#hard-mode--chapters-3-8-and-9). Then when you get to your third pass through Chapter 9 (in Hard Mode) use the answers that currently appear in [Three More Dresses](#three-more-dresses).

The table below summarizes those answers:

|     |     |     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
|  | Old Version | New Version |
| Question | Three More Dresses | DttN | Three More Dresses | DttN |
| 1\. Describe Tifa to Sam | She's Great at Handling the Books | A Great Fighter | A Great Fighter | She's Great at Handling the Books |
| 2\. Coin Flip | No Deal | Tails | Tails | No Deal |
| 3\. Massage choice | Luxury Course | Standard Course | Standard Course | Luxury Course |
| 4\. Describe Aerith's Clothing | It matter what I think? | Comfortable | Comfortable | It matter what I think? |

In case you're curious, I changed the order of events so that when you tackled Chapter 9 in Hard Mode, you would undertake the Side Quests that, when completed in Hard Mode, earn you 2 of Cloud's Manuscripts. I think that this is a definite improvement in the guide, even if it causes some inconvenience to some readers who were in the middle of a run when I submitted the revision. To those of you adversely affected by these changes, I extend my sincere apologies.

## Closing Credits and Comments

First, before I lose your attention completely by rambling on as I tend to do, I want to thank you for taking this journey with me. There are many guides available for this game, and if you are reading this, then you probably made it all the way through to the end with me. I admire your perseverance and your tolerance for reading page after page of my writing, seemingly without end.

For me, a guide like this is a labor of love. I only write guides about games that I enjoy, and only when I believe that I have a specific and unique vision of what I want to convey. I try to give you something that you can't get in any other single guide. Even before finishing FFVIIR, I saw the need for a guide that organized the many tasks that must be completed in order to earn the Platinum Trophy. I then invested the time, energy, and research necessary to bring you, the reader, the very best representation of this vision that I could. While this guide is not without gaps, I aimed at making it as comprehensive, helpful, and ultimately readable as I could. I hope that you agree that I met or at least came close to this goal.

If you have suggestions for future edits or if you just want to tell me how much you enjoyed this guide, please feel free to contact me at

auron402@gmail.com

I readily acknowledge that I contributed very few original ideas to this guide. Almost everything in this guide was first culled from a variety of sources on the Internet and then verified by me when I played through the game while writing the guide. I offer below an admittedly incomplete list of sources that I used. For the record, nothing was taken verbatim from any of these sources. Everything that I got from them was checked against other sources and then rewritten in my own words. I want to express my sincere appreciation for all the work done by others in creating these other sources and offer my apologies to anyone I may have inadvertently omitted.

- Final Fantasy.fandom.com, which is my default source for gaining information and for confirming information found on other sites. It is the single most reliable source I found.
- Gamerant.com
- Powerpyx.com
- Game8.co
- Ign.com
- PSNprofiles.com
- finalfantasy7remake.wiki.fextralife.com
- Gameranx.com, where I got a lot of information about earning Proficiency Bonuses.
- Andrew Testa, whose guide I used on my first playthrough
- Goldensun, whose guide I used for supplemental information
- Lost\_Nemo10, whose guide has the most thorough and detailed information I have found about enemy stats and characteristics.
- BltzZ, whose YouTube channel offers a wealth of information from basics to detailed strategies for winning tough battles.
- brandon.s.bledsoe, who was kind enough to help me add some information early in the guide that many readers will find useful.

I would also like to offer a word of thanks to the good people at GameFAQs.com for providing me a venue in which I can share my thoughts and findings with others.

Thank you again for your kind attention for such a prolonged period of time. Take care, and happy gaming!
